```
   425→          className={`max-w-full max-h-full shadow-wes border border-border/20 ${variant === 'regular-centered' ? 'rounded' : ''}`}
   426→          style={mediaStyle}
   427→          videoDimensions={imageDimensions ?? undefined}
   428→          onLoadedMetadata={onVideoLoadedMetadata}
   429→          playbackStart={playbackStart}
   430→          playbackEnd={playbackEnd}
   431→        />
   432→      ) : (
   433→        // Image with Canvas Overlays
   434→        // Use a single relative container with the image and canvas both using same centering/constraints
   435→        <div
   436→          ref={dragContainerRef}
   437→          className={`relative w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center ${!isRepositionMode ? 'pointer-events-none' : ''}`}
   438→          style={{
   439→            // Black background for reposition mode (shows where empty areas will be)
   440→            ...(isRepositionMode ? {
   441→              backgroundColor: '#000000',
   442→            } : {}),
   443→            // Enable drag-to-move cursor in reposition mode
   444→            cursor: isRepositionMode
   445→              ? (isRepositionDragging ? 'grabbing' : 'grab')
   446→              : undefined,
   447→            // Prevent text selection during drag
   448→            userSelect: isRepositionMode ? 'none' : undefined,
   449→            WebkitUserSelect: isRepositionMode ? 'none' : undefined,
   450→            touchAction: isRepositionMode ? 'none' : undefined,
   451→          }}
   452→          // Apply drag handlers in reposition mode (wheel is native via useEffect)
   453→          {...(isRepositionMode && repositionDragHandlers ? {
   454→            onPointerDown: repositionDragHandlers.onPointerDown,
   455→            onPointerMove: repositionDragHandlers.onPointerMove,
   456→            onPointerUp: repositionDragHandlers.onPointerUp,
   457→            onPointerCancel: repositionDragHandlers.onPointerCancel,
   458→          } : {})}
   459→        >
   460→          {/*
   461→            Wrapper fills available space. Image is constrained within via max-w/h.
   462→            Konva overlay is positioned absolutely at the image's exact location.
   463→          */}
   464→          <div
   465→            ref={imageWrapperRef}
   466→            className={`relative w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center overflow-hidden ${!isRepositionMode ? 'pointer-events-none' : ''}`}
   467→          >
   468→            {/* Use thumbnail or full image based on loading state */}
   469→            <img
   470→              ref={imageRef}
   471→              src={thumbUrl && thumbUrl !== effectiveImageUrl && !fullImageLoaded ? thumbUrl : effectiveImageUrl}
   472→              alt="Media content"
   473→              draggable={false}
   474→              className={`
   475→                block max-w-full max-h-full select-none
   476→                ${variant === 'regular-centered' ? 'rounded' : ''}
   477→                ${isFlippedHorizontally ? 'scale-x-[-1]' : ''}
   478→                ${isSaving ? 'opacity-30' : 'opacity-100'}
   479→                ${isInpaintMode ? 'pointer-events-none' : ''}
   480→                ${editMode !== 'reposition' ? 'transition-opacity duration-300' : ''}
   481→                ${className}
   482→              `.trim()}
   483→              style={{
   484→                ...mediaStyle,
   485→                ...(editMode === 'reposition' && repositionTransformStyle ? repositionTransformStyle : {}),
   486→                transform: editMode === 'reposition' && repositionTransformStyle?.transform
   487→                  ? repositionTransformStyle.transform
   488→                  : (isFlippedHorizontally ? 'scaleX(-1)' : 'none'),
   489→                transformOrigin: editMode === 'reposition' ? 'center center' : undefined,
   490→                pointerEvents: isInpaintMode ? 'none' : 'auto',
   491→                // Keep image below settings panel during reposition (z-80 is the panel)
   492→                zIndex: editMode === 'reposition' ? 40 : undefined,
   493→                position: editMode === 'reposition' ? 'relative' : undefined,
   494→                // STABILITY FIX: Prevent small thumbnail display
   495→                // When showing thumbnail (not full image), force it to fill the container
   496→                // This prevents the jarring size jump when the full image loads
   497→                // NOTE: Only do this for thumbnails - full images need natural sizing for Konva overlay accuracy
   498→                ...((imageDimensions && !fullImageLoaded && thumbUrl && thumbUrl !== effectiveImageUrl) ? {
   499→                  // Thumbnail mode: force full size display
   500→                  width: '100%',
   501→                  height: '100%',
   502→                  objectFit: 'contain' as const,
   503→                } : {}),
   504→                // Always set aspectRatio when we have dimensions (helps with sizing calculations)
   505→                aspectRatio: imageDimensions
   506→                  ? `${imageDimensions.width} / ${imageDimensions.height}`
   507→                  : undefined,
   508→              }}
   509→              onLoad={(e) => {
   510→                const img = e.target as HTMLImageElement;
   511→                // Call onImageLoad for both thumbnail and full image to set dimensions immediately
   512→                // This prevents size jump by setting CSS aspectRatio from thumbnail dimensions
   513→                if (img.src === effectiveImageUrl || !thumbUrl || thumbUrl === effectiveImageUrl) {
   514→                  setFullImageLoaded(true);
   515→                  onImageLoad?.({
   516→                    width: img.naturalWidth,
   517→                    height: img.naturalHeight
   518→                  });
   519→                } else {
   520→                  // Thumbnail loaded - still call onImageLoad to set aspect ratio immediately
   521→                  // This prevents the thumbnail from displaying smaller than the final image
   522→                  onImageLoad?.({
   523→                    width: img.naturalWidth,
   524→                    height: img.naturalHeight
   525→                  });
   526→                }
   527→              }}
   528→              onError={() => {
   529→                normalizeAndPresentError(new Error(`Image load failed: ${effectiveImageUrl}`), {
   530→                  context: `${debugContext}.onImageError`,
   531→                  showToast: false,
   532→                  logData: { url: effectiveImageUrl },
   533→                });
   534→                setImageLoadError(true);
   535→              }}
   536→            />
   537→
   538→            {/* Overlay container - positioned exactly over the image */}
   539→            {isInpaintMode && (editMode === 'inpaint' || editMode === 'annotate') &&
   540→             imageDimensions && onStrokeComplete && onStrokesChange && onSelectionChange && displaySize.width > 0 && displaySize.height > 0 && (
   541→              <div
   542→                className="absolute overflow-hidden"
   543→                style={{
   544→                  zIndex: 50,
   545→                  // Position exactly over the image element
   546→                  left: imageOffset.left,
   547→                  top: imageOffset.top,
   548→                  width: displaySize.width,
   549→                  height: displaySize.height,
   550→                  // CRITICAL: Override parent's pointer-events:none to allow touch/pointer events on canvas
   551→                  pointerEvents: 'auto',
   552→                }}
   553→              >
   554→                <StrokeOverlay
   555→                  ref={strokeOverlayRef}
   556→                  imageWidth={imageDimensions.width}
   557→                  imageHeight={imageDimensions.height}
   558→                  displayWidth={displaySize.width}
   559→                  displayHeight={displaySize.height}
   560→                  strokes={brushStrokes}
   561→                  isEraseMode={isEraseMode}
   562→                  brushSize={brushSize}
   563→                  annotationMode={annotationMode}
   564→                  isInpaintMode={isInpaintMode}
   565→                  isAnnotateMode={isAnnotateMode}
   566→                  editMode={editMode as 'text' | 'inpaint' | 'annotate' | 'reposition' | 'img2img'}
   567→                  onStrokeComplete={onStrokeComplete}
   568→                  onStrokesChange={onStrokesChange}
   569→                  onSelectionChange={onSelectionChange}
   570→                  onTextModeHint={onTextModeHint}
   571→                />
   572→              </div>
   573→            )}
   574→          </div>
   575→
   576→          {/* Preload full image in background when showing thumbnail */}
   577→          {thumbUrl && thumbUrl !== effectiveImageUrl && !fullImageLoaded && (
   578→            <img
   579→              src={effectiveImageUrl}
   580→              alt=""
   581→              className="hidden"
   582→              onLoad={(e) => {
   583→                const img = e.target as HTMLImageElement;
   584→                setFullImageLoaded(true);
   585→                onImageLoad?.({
   586→                  width: img.naturalWidth,
   587→                  height: img.naturalHeight
   588→                });
   589→              }}
   590→              onError={() => {
   591→                normalizeAndPresentError(new Error(`Full image preload failed: ${effectiveImageUrl}`), {
   592→                  context: `${debugContext}.onPreloadError`,
   593→                  showToast: false,
   594→                  logData: { url: effectiveImageUrl },
   595→                });
   596→                // Still try to show thumbnail
   597→              }}
   598→            />
   599→          )}
   600→
   601→          {/* Reposition mode overlays (bounds outline, zoom buttons, rotation handles) */}
   602→          {isRepositionMode && (
   603→            <RepositionOverlay
   604→              displaySize={displaySize}
   605→              imageOffset={imageOffset}
   606→              repositionTransformStyle={repositionTransformStyle}
   607→              repositionScale={repositionScale}
   608→              variant={variant}
   609→              handlesOverlayRef={handlesOverlayRef}
   610→              onRepositionScaleChange={onRepositionScaleChange}
   611→              onRepositionRotationChange={onRepositionRotationChange}
   612→              handleCornerRotateStart={handleCornerRotateStart}
   613→              handleCornerRotateMove={handleCornerRotateMove}
   614→              handleCornerRotateEnd={handleCornerRotateEnd}
   615→            />
   616→          )}
   617→
   618→          {/* Saving State Overlay */}
   619→          {isSaving && (
   620→            <div className={`absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center z-10 bg-black/50 backdrop-blur-sm ${variant === 'regular-centered' ? 'rounded' : ''}`}>
   621→              <div className="text-center text-white bg-black/80 rounded-lg p-4 backdrop-blur-sm border border-white/20">
   622→                <div className={`animate-spin rounded-full border-b-2 border-white mx-auto ${variant === 'mobile-stacked' ? 'h-10 w-10 mb-2' : 'h-12 w-12 mb-3'}`}></div>
   623→                <p className={`font-medium ${variant === 'mobile-stacked' ? 'text-base' : 'text-lg'}`}>Saving flipped image...</p>
   624→                <p className={`text-white/70 mt-1 ${variant === 'mobile-stacked' ? 'text-xs' : 'text-sm'}`}>Please wait</p>
   625→              </div>
   626→            </div>
   627→          )}
   628→
   629→        </div>
   630→      )}
   631→    </div>
   632→  );
   633→};
   634→
```

```
1→/**
     2→ * VideoEditModeDisplay Component
     3→ *
     4→ * Displays video with timeline overlay for portion selection (regenerate mode).
     5→ * Video is paused by default and follows timeline marker position.
     6→ *
     7→ * Used in desktop layout when in video edit/regenerate mode.
     8→ */
     9→
    10→import React, { useState, useEffect, useMemo } from 'react';
    11→import { Plus, Play, Pause, Trash2 } from 'lucide-react';
    12→import { MultiPortionTimeline, formatTime } from '@/shared/components/VideoPortionTimeline';
    13→import { cn } from '@/shared/components/ui/contracts/cn';
    14→import { SEGMENT_OVERLAY_COLORS } from '@/shared/lib/segmentColors';
    15→import { safePlay } from '@/shared/lib/media/safePlay';
    16→
    17→interface VideoEditModeDisplayProps {
    18→  /** Reference to the video element */
    19→  videoRef: React.RefObject<HTMLVideoElement>;
    20→
    21→  /** Video source URL */
    22→  videoUrl: string;
    23→
    24→  /** Poster/thumbnail URL */
    25→  posterUrl?: string;
    26→
    27→  /** Current video duration (used for timeline visibility) */
    28→  videoDuration: number;
    29→
    30→  /** Callback when video metadata loads */
    31→  onLoadedMetadata: (duration: number) => void;
    32→
    33→  /** Selections for the timeline */
    34→  selections: Array<{
    35→    id: string;
    36→    start: number;
    37→    end: number;
    38→    prompt?: string;
    39→  }>;
    40→
    41→  /** Currently active selection ID */
    42→  activeSelectionId: string | null;
    43→
    44→  /** Callback when a selection is changed */
    45→  onSelectionChange: (id: string, start: number, end: number) => void;
    46→
    47→  /** Callback when a selection is clicked */
    48→  onSelectionClick: (id: string | null) => void;
    49→
    50→  /** Callback to remove a selection */
    51→  onRemoveSelection: (id: string) => void;
    52→
    53→  /** Callback to add a new selection */
    54→  onAddSelection: () => void;
    55→
    56→  /** Video FPS for frame number calculations */
    57→  fps?: number;
    58→}
    59→
    60→export const VideoEditModeDisplay: React.FC<VideoEditModeDisplayProps> = ({
    61→  videoRef,
    62→  videoUrl,
    63→  posterUrl,
    64→  videoDuration,
    65→  onLoadedMetadata,
    66→  selections,
    67→  activeSelectionId,
    68→  onSelectionChange,
    69→  onSelectionClick,
    70→  onRemoveSelection,
    71→  onAddSelection,
    72→  fps = 16,
    73→}) => {
    74→  // Track current video time for overlay display
    75→  const [currentVideoTime, setCurrentVideoTime] = useState(0);
    76→  const [isPlaying, setIsPlaying] = useState(false);
    77→
    78→  // Track video time updates
    79→  useEffect(() => {
    80→    const video = videoRef.current;
    81→    if (!video) return;
    82→
    83→    const handleTimeUpdate = () => setCurrentVideoTime(video.currentTime);
    84→    const handlePlay = () => setIsPlaying(true);
    85→    const handlePause = () => setIsPlaying(false);
    86→
    87→    video.addEventListener('timeupdate', handleTimeUpdate);
    88→    video.addEventListener('play', handlePlay);
    89→    video.addEventListener('pause', handlePause);
    90→    return () => {
    91→      video.removeEventListener('timeupdate', handleTimeUpdate);
    92→      video.removeEventListener('play', handlePlay);
    93→      video.removeEventListener('pause', handlePause);
    94→    };
    95→  }, [videoRef]);
    96→
    97→  // Check if current time is in a regeneration zone and which segment
    98→  const regenerationZoneInfo = useMemo(() => {
    99→    const sortedSelections = [...selections].sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start);
   100→    for (let i = 0; i < sortedSelections.length; i++) {
   101→      const selection = sortedSelections[i];
   102→      if (currentVideoTime >= selection.start && currentVideoTime <= selection.end) {
   103→        return { inZone: true, segmentIndex: i, selectionId: selection.id };
   104→      }
   105→    }
   106→    return { inZone: false, segmentIndex: -1, selectionId: null };
   107→  }, [currentVideoTime, selections]);
   108→
   109→  const handleLoadedMetadata = (e: React.SyntheticEvent<HTMLVideoElement>) => {
   110→    const video = e.currentTarget;
   111→    if (Number.isFinite(video.duration) && video.duration > 0) {
   112→      onLoadedMetadata(video.duration);
   113→      // Seek to start of first selection when video loads
   114→      if (selections.length > 0 && selections[0].start > 0) {
   115→        video.currentTime = selections[0].start;
   116→      }
   117→    }
   118→  };
   119→
   120→  // Poll for video metadata - handles race conditions and cached videos
   121→  useEffect(() => {
   122→    // If we already have duration, no need to poll
   123→    if (videoDuration > 0) {
   124→      return;
   125→    }
   126→
   127→    let cancelled = false;
   128→
   129→    const checkVideoReady = () => {
   130→      const video = videoRef.current;
   131→      if (video && Number.isFinite(video.duration) && video.duration > 0) {
   132→        onLoadedMetadata(video.duration);
   133→        return true;
   134→      }
   135→      return false;
   136→    };
   137→
   138→    // Poll every 50ms until video is ready (max 3 seconds)
   139→    let attempts = 0;
   140→    const maxAttempts = 60;
   141→
   142→    const poll = () => {
   143→      if (cancelled) return;
   144→      if (checkVideoReady()) return;
   145→      attempts++;
   146→      if (attempts < maxAttempts) {
   147→        setTimeout(poll, 50);
   148→      }
   149→    };
   150→
   151→    poll();
   152→
   153→    return () => {
   154→      cancelled = true;
   155→    };
   156→  }, [videoRef, onLoadedMetadata, videoDuration]);
   157→
   158→  const handlePlayPause = () => {
   159→    const video = videoRef.current;
   160→    if (!video || !videoUrl) return;
   161→    if (video.paused) {
   162→      void safePlay(video, 'VideoEditModeDisplay.handlePlayPause', {
   163→        videoUrl,
   164→      });
   165→    } else {
   166→      video.pause();
   167→    }
   168→  };
   169→
   170→  // Render video directly without wrapper - parent handles centering
   171→  // Overlays are positioned relative to parent container
   172→  return (
   173→    <>
   174→      <video
   175→        ref={videoRef}
   176→        src={videoUrl || undefined}
   177→        poster={posterUrl}
   178→        muted
   179→        autoPlay={!!videoUrl}
   180→        playsInline
   181→        controls={false}
   182→        preload="auto"
   183→        className="max-w-full max-h-full object-contain shadow-wes border border-border/20 rounded cursor-pointer"
   184→        style={{ WebkitAppearance: 'none' }}
   185→        onLoadedMetadata={handleLoadedMetadata}
   186→        onClick={handlePlayPause}
   187→      />
   188→
   189→      {/* Position overlay - top left */}
   190→      {videoDuration > 0 && (
   191→        <div className="absolute top-3 left-3 z-10 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] font-mono text-white/80 bg-black/50 backdrop-blur-sm rounded px-2 py-1">
   192→          {formatTime(currentVideoTime)}
   193→          <span className="text-white/50 ml-1">f{Math.round(currentVideoTime * fps)}</span>
   194→          {' '}
   195→          <span className={cn(
   196→            "px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] ml-1",
   197→            regenerationZoneInfo.inZone
   198→              ? SEGMENT_OVERLAY_COLORS[regenerationZoneInfo.segmentIndex % SEGMENT_OVERLAY_COLORS.length].bg + ' ' + SEGMENT_OVERLAY_COLORS[regenerationZoneInfo.segmentIndex % SEGMENT_OVERLAY_COLORS.length].text
   199→              : "bg-white/20 text-white/70"
   200→          )}>
   201→            {regenerationZoneInfo.inZone ? `segment ${regenerationZoneInfo.segmentIndex + 1}` : 'keep'}
   202→          </span>
   203→          {/* Delete button - only show when in a segment and there's more than 1 */}
   204→          {regenerationZoneInfo.inZone && selections.length > 1 && regenerationZoneInfo.selectionId && (
   205→            <button
   206→              onClick={() => onRemoveSelection(regenerationZoneInfo.selectionId!)}
   207→              className="ml-1 p-0.5 rounded hover:bg-white/20 text-white/50 hover:text-white transition-colors"
   208→            >
   209→              <Trash2 className="w-3 h-3" />
   210→            </button>
   211→          )}
   212→        </div>
   213→      )}
   214→
   215→      {/* Play/Pause button overlay - center */}
   216→      <button
   217→        onClick={handlePlayPause}
   218→        className="absolute top-1/2 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 z-10 w-16 h-16 flex items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-black/50 text-white/80 hover:bg-black/70 hover:text-white transition-all opacity-0 hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100"
   219→        style={{ marginTop: '-70px' }} // Offset for timeline at bottom
   220→      >
   221→        {isPlaying ? <Pause className="w-8 h-8" /> : <Play className="w-8 h-8 ml-1" />}
   222→      </button>
   223→
   224→      {/* Timeline overlay for portion selection */}
   225→      {videoDuration > 0 && (
   226→        <div className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 px-4 pb-4 z-20">
   227→          <div className="bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm rounded-lg p-3">
   228→            <MultiPortionTimeline
   229→              duration={videoDuration}
   230→              selections={selections}
   231→              activeSelectionId={activeSelectionId}
   232→              onSelectionChange={onSelectionChange}
   233→              onSelectionClick={onSelectionClick}
   234→              onRemoveSelection={onRemoveSelection}
   235→              videoRef={videoRef}
   236→              videoUrl={videoUrl}
   237→              fps={fps}
   238→            />
   239→
   240→            {/* Add selection button */}
   241→            <button
   242→              onClick={onAddSelection}
   243→              className="mt-2 flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-white/70 hover:text-white transition-colors"
   244→            >
   245→              <Plus className="w-3 h-3" />
   246→              Add another portion
   247→            </button>
   248→          </div>
   249→        </div>
   250→      )}
   251→    </>
   252→  );
   253→};
   254→
```

```
1→/**
     2→ * VideoTrimModeDisplay Component
     3→ *
     4→ * Displays video that plays within the trimmed region and loops automatically.
     5→ * Constrains playback to the "keep" region (startTrim to duration - endTrim).
     6→ *
     7→ * Used in desktop and mobile layouts when in video trim mode.
     8→ */
     9→
    10→import React from 'react';
    11→import type { TrimState } from '@/shared/types/videoTrim';
    12→
    13→interface VideoTrimModeDisplayProps {
    14→  /** Reference to the video element */
    15→  videoRef: React.RefObject<HTMLVideoElement>;
    16→
    17→  /** Video source URL */
    18→  videoUrl: string;
    19→
    20→  /** Poster/thumbnail URL */
    21→  posterUrl?: string;
    22→
    23→  /** Current trim state */
    24→  trimState: TrimState;
    25→
    26→  /** Callback when video metadata loads */
    27→  onLoadedMetadata: (duration: number) => void;
    28→
    29→  /** Callback on time update (for syncing current time display) */
    30→  onTimeUpdate: (currentTime: number) => void;
    31→
    32→  /** Additional className for the video element */
    33→  className?: string;
    34→}
    35→
    36→export const VideoTrimModeDisplay: React.FC<VideoTrimModeDisplayProps> = ({
    37→  videoRef,
    38→  videoUrl,
    39→  posterUrl,
    40→  trimState,
    41→  onLoadedMetadata,
    42→  onTimeUpdate,
    43→  className = "max-w-full max-h-full object-contain shadow-wes border border-border/20 rounded",
    44→}) => {
    45→  const handleLoadedMetadata = (e: React.SyntheticEvent<HTMLVideoElement>) => {
    46→    const video = e.currentTarget;
    47→    if (Number.isFinite(video.duration) && video.duration > 0) {
    48→      onLoadedMetadata(video.duration);
    49→      // Seek to start of keep region when video loads
    50→      video.currentTime = trimState.startTrim;
    51→    }
    52→  };
    53→
    54→  const handleTimeUpdate = (e: React.SyntheticEvent<HTMLVideoElement>) => {
    55→    const video = e.currentTarget;
    56→    onTimeUpdate(video.currentTime);
    57→
    58→    // Constrain playback to keep region (startTrim to duration - endTrim)
    59→    const keepStart = trimState.startTrim;
    60→    const keepEnd = trimState.videoDuration - trimState.endTrim;
    61→
    62→    if (video.currentTime >= keepEnd) {
    63→      // Loop back to start of keep region and ensure playing
    64→      video.currentTime = keepStart;
    65→      if (video.paused) {
    66→        video.play().catch(() => {});
    67→      }
    68→    } else if (video.currentTime < keepStart) {
    69→      // Jump to start of keep region if before it
    70→      video.currentTime = keepStart;
    71→      if (video.paused) {
    72→        video.play().catch(() => {});
    73→      }
    74→    }
    75→  };
    76→
    77→  const handleEnded = (e: React.SyntheticEvent<HTMLVideoElement>) => {
    78→    // If video reaches the actual end, loop back to keep region start
    79→    const video = e.currentTarget;
    80→    video.currentTime = trimState.startTrim;
    81→    video.play().catch(() => {});
    82→  };
    83→
    84→  return (
    85→    <video
    86→      ref={videoRef}
    87→      src={videoUrl}
    88→      poster={posterUrl}
    89→      muted
    90→      playsInline
    91→      controls
    92→      autoPlay
    93→      loop={false}
    94→      preload="auto"
    95→      className={className}

    97→      onTimeUpdate={handleTimeUpdate}
    98→      onEnded={handleEnded}
    99→    />

   101→};
```

```
   159→  // When posterOnlyUntilClick is enabled, defer activation until interaction
   160→  const [isActivated, setIsActivated] = useState<boolean>(() => !posterOnlyUntilClick);
   161→  const speedOptions = [0.25, 0.5, 1, 1.5, 2];
   162→  
   163→  // Safely handle potentially missing or placeholder sources
   164→  const displaySrc = getDisplayUrl(src);
   165→  const hasValidVideoSrc = displaySrc && displaySrc !== '/placeholder.svg';
   166→  
   167→  const stableSrcRef = useRef<string>(stripQueryParameters(src));
   168→  
   169→  const handleMouseMove = useCallback((e: React.MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
   170→    // Skip hover interactions on mobile devices or when scrubbing is disabled
   171→    if (!scrubbingEnabled) {
   172→      return;
   173→    }
   174→
   175→    if (loadOnDemand && !hasLoadedOnDemand) {
   176→      setHasLoadedOnDemand(true);
   177→    }
   178→
   179→    // Prime video loading if needed
   180→    if (videoRef.current && preloadProp === 'none' && videoRef.current.readyState < 2) {
   181→      try {
   182→        videoRef.current.load();
   183→      } catch { /* intentionally ignored */ }
   184→    }
   185→
   186→    // Delegate to the hook's handler for actual scrubbing
   187→    handleScrubMouseMove(e);
   188→  }, [scrubbingEnabled, loadOnDemand, hasLoadedOnDemand, preloadProp, handleScrubMouseMove, videoRef]);
   189→
   190→  const handleMouseEnter = useCallback(() => {
   191→    // Handle autoplayOnHover mode separately
   192→    if (autoplayOnHover && !isMobile && !disableScrubbing) {
   193→      if (videoRef.current) {
   194→        void safePlay(videoRef.current, 'HoverScrubVideo.autoplayOnHover', {
   195→          src: getDisplayUrl(src),
   196→        });
   197→      }
   198→      return;
   199→    }
   200→
   201→    // Skip if scrubbing not enabled
   202→    if (!scrubbingEnabled) {
   203→      return;
   204→    }
   205→
   206→    isHoveringRef.current = true;
   207→
   208→    // Prime video loading if needed
   209→    if (videoRef.current && videoRef.current.readyState === 0) {
   210→      try {
   211→        videoRef.current.load();
   212→      } catch { /* intentionally ignored */ }
   213→    }
   214→
   215→    // Delegate to the hook's handler
   216→    handleScrubMouseEnter();
   217→  }, [scrubbingEnabled, autoplayOnHover, isMobile, disableScrubbing, handleScrubMouseEnter, src, videoRef]);
   218→
   219→  const handleMouseLeave = useCallback(() => {
   220→    // Handle autoplayOnHover mode separately
   221→    if (autoplayOnHover && !isMobile && !disableScrubbing) {
   222→      if (videoRef.current) {
   223→        videoRef.current.pause();
   224→        videoRef.current.currentTime = 0;
   225→      }
   226→      return;
   227→    }
   228→
   229→    // Skip if scrubbing not enabled
   230→    if (!scrubbingEnabled) {
   231→      return;
   232→    }
   233→
   234→    isHoveringRef.current = false;
   235→
   236→    // Delegate to the hook's handler
   237→    handleScrubMouseLeave();
   238→  }, [scrubbingEnabled, autoplayOnHover, isMobile, disableScrubbing, handleScrubMouseLeave, videoRef]);
   239→
   240→  const handleSpeedChange = (speed: number) => {
   241→    if (videoRef.current) {
   242→      videoRef.current.playbackRate = speed;
   243→      setPlaybackRate(speed);
   244→    }
   245→  };
   246→
   247→  const handleLoadedMetadata = useCallback(() => {
   248→    // Let the hook handle duration and scrubber recalculation
   249→    handleScrubLoadedMetadata();
   250→
   251→    if (!videoRef.current) return;
   252→
   253→    // Ensure video is paused first to prevent autoplay
   254→    if (!videoRef.current.paused) {
   255→      videoRef.current.pause();
   256→    }
   257→
   258→    // Set to first frame to show as poster - but only for gallery thumbnails, not lightbox
   259→    // IMPORTANT: Skip this if we already have a poster to avoid "weird reset" flashes
   260→    if (!disableScrubbing && videoRef.current.currentTime === 0 && !poster) {
   261→      videoRef.current.currentTime = 0.001;
   262→    }
   263→  }, [disableScrubbing, poster, handleScrubLoadedMetadata, videoRef]);
   264→
   265→  useEffect(() => {
   266→    if (thumbnailMode) {
   267→      // Skip attaching event listeners entirely in thumbnail mode
   268→      return;
   269→    }
   270→    const video = videoRef.current;
   271→    if (!video) return;
   272→
   273→    const currentStableSrc = stripQueryParameters(src);
   274→    const isActuallyNewSrc = currentStableSrc !== stableSrcRef.current;
   275→    
   276→    // Update stable source ref
   277→    stableSrcRef.current = currentStableSrc;
   278→
   279→    // Only perform reset logic if the underlying source has actually changed
   280→    if (isActuallyNewSrc) {
   281→      // Ensure the video starts paused
   282→      video.pause();
   283→      // Important: never force-reset currentTime in lightbox (disableScrubbing=true)
   284→      if (!disableScrubbing) {
   285→        // Only reset currentTime for gallery thumbnails
   286→        video.currentTime = 0;
   287→      }
   288→      // Reset scrubbing state for new source
   289→      resetScrubbing();
   290→    }
   291→
   292→    // Add event listeners to track unexpected play events
   293→    const handlePlay = () => {
   294→      // Only enforce anti-autoplay on mobile thumbnails (scrubbing enabled)
   295→      // but allow if explicitly user-initiated (click/touch)
   296→      if (!disableScrubbing && isMobile && !isHoveringRef.current && !userInitiatedPlayRef.current) {
   297→        video.pause();
   298→      }
   299→    };
   300→
   301→    const handlePause = () => {
   302→      // Reset user-initiated flag on pause so future autoplays are blocked again
   303→      userInitiatedPlayRef.current = false;
   304→    };
   305→
   306→    const handleSeeked = () => {
   307→      
   308→      // Only enforce pause-after-seek on mobile thumbnails, unless user initiated
   309→      if (!disableScrubbing && isMobile && !userInitiatedPlayRef.current && !video.paused) {
   310→        video.pause();
   311→      }
   312→    };
   313→
   314→    video.addEventListener('play', handlePlay);
   315→    video.addEventListener('pause', handlePause);
   316→    video.addEventListener('seeked', handleSeeked);
   317→
   318→    return () => {
   319→      if (video) {
   320→        video.removeEventListener('play', handlePlay);
   321→        video.removeEventListener('pause', handlePause);
   322→        video.removeEventListener('seeked', handleSeeked);
   323→        video.pause();
   324→      }
   325→    };
   326→  // NOTE: scrubbing.reset is a stable callback, no need to include the entire scrubbing object
   327→  // Including scrubbing would cause cleanup to run on every state change (e.g., isPlaying),
   328→  // which would pause the video immediately after play() is called
   329→  }, [src, isMobile, disableScrubbing, thumbnailMode, resetScrubbing, videoRef]);
   330→
   331→  // Additional mobile protection - use Intersection Observer to detect when video becomes visible
   332→  // Only for gallery thumbnails, not lightbox
   333→  useEffect(() => {
   334→    if (!isMobile || !videoRef.current || disableScrubbing || thumbnailMode) return;
   335→
   336→    const video = videoRef.current;
   337→    
   338→    const observer = new IntersectionObserver(
   339→      (entries) => {
   340→        entries.forEach((entry) => {
   341→          if (entry.isIntersecting) {
   342→            // Ensure video thumbnail is paused when it comes into view on mobile
   343→            if (!video.paused) {
   344→              video.pause();
   345→            }
   346→          }
   347→        });
   348→      },
   349→      { threshold: 0.1 }
   350→    );
   351→
   352→    observer.observe(video);
   353→
   354→    return () => {
   355→      observer.disconnect();
   356→    };
   357→  }, [isMobile, disableScrubbing, thumbnailMode, videoRef]);
   358→
   359→  // Periodic mobile check to catch any unexpected play states - but only for gallery thumbnails
   360→  useEffect(() => {
   361→    if (!isMobile || disableScrubbing || thumbnailMode) return;
   362→
   363→    const intervalId = setInterval(() => {
   364→      if (videoRef.current && !videoRef.current.paused) {
   365→        videoRef.current.pause();
   366→      }
   367→    }, 1000);
   368→
   369→    return () => {
   370→      clearInterval(intervalId);
   371→    };
   372→  }, [isMobile, disableScrubbing, thumbnailMode, videoRef]);
   373→
   374→  return (
   375→    <div
   376→      ref={containerRef}
   377→      className={cn('relative group', className)}
   378→      onMouseEnter={isMobile || disableScrubbing || thumbnailMode ? undefined : handleMouseEnter}
   379→      onMouseLeave={isMobile || disableScrubbing || thumbnailMode ? undefined : handleMouseLeave}
   380→      onMouseMove={isMobile || disableScrubbing || thumbnailMode ? undefined : handleMouseMove}
   381→      {...rest}
   382→    >
   383→      <video
   384→        ref={videoRefCallback}
   385→        src={hasValidVideoSrc ? displaySrc : undefined}
   386→        poster={poster ? getDisplayUrl(poster) : undefined}
   387→        preload={thumbnailMode ? 'none' : (isMobile ? 'metadata' : preloadProp)}
   388→        controls={showNativeControls}
   389→        onLoadedMetadata={handleLoadedMetadata}
   390→        loop={loop}
   391→        muted={muted}
   392→        autoPlay={false}
   393→        playsInline
   394→        className={cn('w-full h-full object-contain', videoClassName, {
   395→          'hide-video-controls': !showNativeControls
   396→        })}
   397→        onDoubleClick={onDoubleClick}
   398→        onTouchEnd={(e) => {
   399→          onTouchEnd?.(e);
   400→        }}
   401→        onLoadStart={(e) => {
   402→          onLoadStart?.(e);
   403→        }}
   404→        onLoadedData={(e) => {
   405→          onLoadedData?.(e);
   406→        }}
   407→        onCanPlay={() => {
   408→          // Prevent autoplay on mobile only for gallery thumbnails (scrubbing enabled)
   409→          if (!disableScrubbing && isMobile && videoRef.current && !videoRef.current.paused) {
   410→            videoRef.current.pause();
   411→          }
   412→        }}
   413→        onError={(e) => {
   414→          normalizeAndPresentError(new Error('[HoverScrubVideo] Video error occurred'), {
   415→            context: 'HoverScrubVideo',
   416→            showToast: false,
   417→            logData: {
   418→              isMobile,
   419→              src: getDisplayUrl(src),
   420→              error: e.currentTarget.error,
   421→              posterSrc: poster ? getDisplayUrl(poster) : 'none',
   422→            },
   423→          });
   424→          onVideoError?.(e);
   425→        }}
   426→      >
   427→        Your browser does not support the video tag.
   428→      </video>
   429→
   430→      {/* Overlay play hint when deferring src */}
   431→      {posterOnlyUntilClick && !isActivated && (
   432→        <div
   433→          className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/10 hover:bg-black/20 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
   434→          onClick={() => setIsActivated(true)}
   435→        >
   436→          <div className="flex items-center justify-center w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-black/70 text-white text-sm">
   437→            ▶
   438→          </div>
   439→        </div>
   440→      )}
   441→
   442→      {/* Scrubber Line - Desktop only and when scrubbing is enabled */}
   443→      {!isMobile && !disableScrubbing && !thumbnailMode && isActivated && scrubberPosition !== null && (
   444→        <div
   445→          className={cn(
   446→            "absolute top-0 bottom-0 w-0.5 bg-white shadow-lg z-30 pointer-events-none transition-opacity duration-300",
   447→            scrubberVisible ? "opacity-100" : "opacity-0"
   448→          )}
   449→          style={{ left: `${scrubberPosition}%` }}
   450→        >
   451→          {/* Scrubber handle/dot */}
   452→          <div className="absolute top-1/2 left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-3 h-3 bg-white rounded-full shadow-lg border-2 border-black/20" />
   453→
   454→          {/* Time indicator */}
   455→          <div className="absolute -top-8 left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 bg-black/80 text-white text-xs px-2 py-1 rounded whitespace-nowrap">
   456→            {Number.isFinite(duration) && duration > 0 && (
   457→              `${Math.floor((scrubberPosition / 100) * duration)}s / ${Math.floor(duration)}s`
   458→            )}
   459→          </div>
   460→        </div>
   461→      )}
   462→
   463→      {/* Progress bar at bottom - shows playback position during hover */}
   464→      {!isMobile && !disableScrubbing && !thumbnailMode && isActivated && scrubberPosition !== null && (
   465→        <div className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 h-1 bg-black/30 z-20 pointer-events-none">
   466→          <div
   467→            className="h-full bg-primary transition-all duration-75"
   468→            style={{ width: `${scrubberPosition}%` }}
   469→          />
   470→        </div>
   471→      )}
   472→
   473→      {/* Speed controls overlay - Desktop only */}
   474→      {!isMobile && !disableScrubbing && !thumbnailMode && isActivated && showSpeedControls && (
   475→        <div 
   476→          className={cn(
   477→            'absolute flex items-center gap-x-1 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 group-touch:opacity-100 transition-opacity bg-black/60 rounded-md px-2 py-1 backdrop-blur-sm z-20',
   478→            speedControlsPosition === 'top-left' 
   479→              ? 'top-2 left-2' 
   480→              : 'bottom-2 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2'
   481→          )}
   482→        >
   483→          {speedOptions.map((speed) => (
   484→            <Button
   485→              key={speed}
   486→              variant={playbackRate === speed ? 'default' : 'secondary'}
   487→              size="sm"
   488→              onClick={(e) => {
   489→                e.stopPropagation();
   490→                handleSpeedChange(speed);
   491→              }}
   492→              className={cn(
   493→                'h-5 min-w-[36px] px-1.5 text-xs',
   494→                playbackRate === speed ? 'text-white' : 'text-foreground'
   495→              )}
   496→            >
   497→              {speed}x
   498→            </Button>
   499→          ))}
   500→        </div>
   501→      )}
   502→    </div>
   503→  );
   504→};
   505→
   506→export default HoverScrubVideo;
   507→
```

```
     5→interface VideoContentProps {
```

```
1→/**
     2→ * InlineSegmentVideo - Individual segment thumbnail in the output strip.
     3→ *
     4→ * The component routes render behavior through focused view modules:
     5→ * - SegmentPlaceholder: no child generation yet
     6→ * - SegmentProcessing: child exists but no output
     7→ * - SegmentPreview: full thumbnail/hover preview UI
     8→ */
     9→
    10→import type React from 'react';
    11→import { SegmentPlaceholder } from './inline-segment-video/SegmentPlaceholder';
    12→import { SegmentPreview } from './inline-segment-video/SegmentPreview';
    13→import { SegmentProcessing } from './inline-segment-video/SegmentProcessing';
    14→import type { InlineSegmentVideoProps } from './inline-segment-video/types';
    15→import { useLayoutProps } from './inline-segment-video/useLayoutProps';
    16→import { useSegmentBadge } from './inline-segment-video/useSegmentBadge';
    17→
    18→export const InlineSegmentVideo: React.FC<InlineSegmentVideoProps> = ({
    19→  slot,
    20→  pairIndex,
    21→  onClick,
    22→  projectAspectRatio,
    23→  isMobile = false,
    24→  leftPercent,
    25→  widthPercent,
    26→  layout = 'absolute',
    27→  compact = false,
    28→  onOpenPairSettings,
    29→  onDelete,
    30→  isDeleting = false,
    31→  isPending = false,
    32→  hasSourceChanged = false,
    33→  isScrubbingActive = false,
    34→  onScrubbingStart,
    35→  scrubbingContainerRef,
    36→  scrubbingContainerProps,
    37→  scrubbingProgress,
    38→  readOnly = false,
    39→}) => {
    40→  const layoutProps = useLayoutProps(layout, compact, isMobile, leftPercent, widthPercent);
    41→
    42→  const isPlaceholder = slot.type === 'placeholder';
    43→  const child = slot.type === 'child' ? slot.child : null;
    44→
    45→  const {
    46→    badgeData,
    47→    showNewBadge,
    48→    isNewWithNoVariants,
    49→    unviewedCount,
    50→    onMarkAllViewed,
    51→  } = useSegmentBadge(child);
    52→
    53→  if (isPlaceholder) {
    54→    return (
    55→      <SegmentPlaceholder
    56→        layoutProps={layoutProps}
    57→        isPending={isPending}
    58→        readOnly={readOnly}
    59→        pairIndex={pairIndex}
    60→        onOpenPairSettings={onOpenPairSettings}
    61→      />
    62→    );
    63→  }
    64→
    65→  if (!child || !child.location) {
    66→    return (
    67→      <SegmentProcessing
    68→        layoutProps={layoutProps}
    69→        isPending={isPending}
    70→        pairIndex={pairIndex}
    71→        onOpenPairSettings={onOpenPairSettings}
    72→      />
    73→    );
    74→  }
    75→
    76→  return (
    77→    <SegmentPreview
    78→      layoutProps={layoutProps}
    79→      child={child}
    80→      pairIndex={pairIndex}
    81→      onClick={onClick}
    82→      projectAspectRatio={projectAspectRatio}
    83→      onDelete={onDelete}
    84→      isDeleting={isDeleting}
    85→      isPending={isPending}
    86→      hasSourceChanged={hasSourceChanged}
    87→      scrubbing={{
    88→        isActive: isScrubbingActive,
    89→        onStart: onScrubbingStart,
    90→        containerRef: scrubbingContainerRef,
    91→        containerProps: scrubbingContainerProps,
    92→        progress: scrubbingProgress,
    93→      }}
    94→      badge={{
    95→        data: badgeData ?? null,
    96→        showNew: showNewBadge,
    97→        isNewWithNoVariants,
    98→        unviewedCount,
    99→        onMarkAllViewed,
   100→      }}
   101→    />
   102→  );
   103→};
   104→
```

```
1→/**
     2→ * StructureVideoPreview Component
     3→ *
     4→ * Extracts and displays 3 frames (start, mid, end) from a structure video.
     5→ *
     6→ * Uses a single async effect with proper cancellation to:
     7→ * 1. Load video metadata
     8→ * 2. Seek to each frame position
     9→ * 3. Capture frame to canvas
    10→ * 4. Notify parent when complete
    11→ */
    12→
    13→import React, { useState, useRef, useMemo, useEffect } from 'react';
    14→import { Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
    15→import { normalizeAndPresentError } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandling/runtimeError';
    16→
    17→interface StructureVideoPreviewProps {
    18→  videoUrl: string;
    19→  frameRange: {
    20→    segmentStart: number;
    21→    segmentEnd: number;
    22→    videoTotalFrames: number;
    23→    videoFps: number;
    24→    /** Video's output start position on timeline (for "fit to range" calculation) */
    25→    videoOutputStart?: number;
    26→    /** Video's output end position on timeline (for "fit to range" calculation) */
    27→    videoOutputEnd?: number;
    28→  };
    29→  /** Treatment mode determines how frames are sampled:
    30→   * - 'adjust' (fit to range): samples the portion of video that maps to this segment
    31→   * - 'clip' (1:1): samples from segment's specific frame range */
    32→  treatment: 'adjust' | 'clip';
    33→  /** Called when all frames have been captured and displayed */
    34→  onLoadComplete?: () => void;
    35→}
    36→
    37→/**
    38→ * Seek a video element to a target time, with a timeout to prevent hanging
    39→ * if the browser doesn't fire 'seeked' (e.g. already at the target time).
    40→ */
    41→function seekVideo(video: HTMLVideoElement, targetTime: number): Promise<void> {
    42→  return new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
    43→    // If already within ~1ms of target, skip the seek entirely
    44→    if (Math.abs(video.currentTime - targetTime) < 0.001) {
    45→      resolve();
    46→      return;
    47→    }
    48→
    49→    const timeout = setTimeout(resolve, 2000);
    50→    video.addEventListener('seeked', () => {
    51→      clearTimeout(timeout);
    52→      resolve();
    53→    }, { once: true });
    54→    video.currentTime = targetTime;
    55→  });
    56→}
    57→
    58→export const StructureVideoPreview: React.FC<StructureVideoPreviewProps> = ({
    59→  videoUrl,
    60→  frameRange,
    61→  treatment,
    62→  onLoadComplete,
    63→}) => {
    64→  const videoRef = useRef<HTMLVideoElement>(null);
    65→  // Stable refs array - doesn't change between renders
    66→  const canvasRefs = useRef<(HTMLCanvasElement | null)[]>([null, null, null]);
    67→  const [capturedCount, setCapturedCount] = useState(0);
    68→  // Track which URL the captures are for - prevents showing stale frames on URL change
    69→  const [capturedForUrl, setCapturedForUrl] = useState<string | null>(null);
    70→  // Track if we're currently extracting (to show loading state without clearing frames)
    71→  const [isExtracting, setIsExtracting] = useState(false);
    72→
    73→  // Calculate the 3 frame positions (start, middle, end)
    74→  // For 'adjust' (fit to range): sample the portion of video that maps to this segment
    75→  // For 'clip' (1:1): sample from segment's specific frame range
    76→  const framePositions = useMemo(() => {
    77→    const { segmentStart, segmentEnd, videoTotalFrames, videoFps, videoOutputStart = 0, videoOutputEnd } = frameRange;
    78→
    79→    let videoFrameStart: number;
    80→    let videoFrameEnd: number;
    81→    let videoFrameMid: number;
    82→
    83→    if (treatment === 'adjust') {
    84→      // "Fit to range" mode: the video is stretched to fit its output range on the timeline
    85→      // Calculate which portion of the video maps to THIS segment's position
    86→      const effectiveVideoOutputEnd = videoOutputEnd ?? segmentEnd;
    87→      const videoOutputRange = effectiveVideoOutputEnd - videoOutputStart;
    88→
    89→      if (videoOutputRange > 0) {
    90→        // Calculate segment's position as a ratio within the video's output range
    91→        const segmentStartRatio = (segmentStart - videoOutputStart) / videoOutputRange;
    92→        const segmentEndRatio = (segmentEnd - videoOutputStart) / videoOutputRange;
    93→
    94→        // Map to video frames
    95→        videoFrameStart = Math.floor(segmentStartRatio * (videoTotalFrames - 1));
    96→        videoFrameEnd = Math.floor(segmentEndRatio * (videoTotalFrames - 1));
    97→      } else {
    98→        // Fallback if range is invalid
    99→        videoFrameStart = 0;
   100→        videoFrameEnd = videoTotalFrames - 1;
   101→      }
   102→
   103→      // Clamp to valid range
   104→      videoFrameStart = Math.max(0, Math.min(videoFrameStart, videoTotalFrames - 1));
   105→      videoFrameEnd = Math.max(0, Math.min(videoFrameEnd, videoTotalFrames - 1));
   106→      videoFrameMid = Math.floor((videoFrameStart + videoFrameEnd) / 2);
   107→    } else {
   108→      // "1:1 mapping" mode: video frame N maps to output frame (videoOutputStart + N)
   109→      // So segment at timeline position X uses video frame (X - videoOutputStart)
   110→      videoFrameStart = segmentStart - videoOutputStart;
   111→      videoFrameEnd = segmentEnd - videoOutputStart;
   112→
   113→      // Clamp to valid range
   114→      videoFrameStart = Math.max(0, Math.min(videoFrameStart, videoTotalFrames - 1));
   115→      videoFrameEnd = Math.max(0, Math.min(videoFrameEnd, videoTotalFrames - 1));
   116→      videoFrameMid = Math.floor((videoFrameStart + videoFrameEnd) / 2);
   117→    }
   118→
   119→    return [
   120→      { frame: videoFrameStart, time: videoFrameStart / videoFps, label: 'Start' },
   121→      { frame: videoFrameMid, time: videoFrameMid / videoFps, label: 'Mid' },
   122→      { frame: videoFrameEnd, time: videoFrameEnd / videoFps, label: 'End' },
   123→    ];
   124→  }, [frameRange, treatment]);
   125→
   126→  // Track previous URL to detect URL changes without adding capturedForUrl to effect deps
   127→  const prevUrlRef = useRef<string | null>(null);
   128→
   129→  // Single effect to handle entire capture flow with proper cancellation
   130→  useEffect(() => {
   131→    const video = videoRef.current;
   132→    if (!video) return;
   133→
   134→    let cancelled = false;
   135→
   136→    // Only clear frames immediately if URL changed (completely different video)
   137→    // For treatment/position changes, keep showing old frames until new ones are ready
   138→    const urlChanged = prevUrlRef.current !== null && prevUrlRef.current !== videoUrl;
   139→    if (urlChanged) {
   140→      setCapturedCount(0);
   141→      setCapturedForUrl(null);
   142→    }
   143→    prevUrlRef.current = videoUrl;
   144→
   145→    setIsExtracting(true);
   146→
   147→    const captureAllFrames = async () => {
   148→      // Wait for video metadata to load
   149→      if (video.readyState < 1) {
   150→        await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
   151→          const onLoad = () => { video.removeEventListener('error', onError); resolve(); };
   152→          const onError = () => { video.removeEventListener('loadedmetadata', onLoad); reject(new Error('Video failed to load')); };
   153→          video.addEventListener('loadedmetadata', onLoad, { once: true });
   154→          video.addEventListener('error', onError, { once: true });
   155→        });
   156→      }
   157→
   158→      if (cancelled) return;
   159→
   160→      // Capture each frame sequentially
   161→      for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
   162→        if (cancelled) return;
   163→
   164→        // Seek to frame position (with timeout to prevent hanging)
   165→        await seekVideo(video, framePositions[i].time);
   166→
   167→        if (cancelled) return;
   168→
   169→        // Capture to canvas
   170→        const canvas = canvasRefs.current[i];
   171→        if (canvas && video.videoWidth) {
   172→          canvas.width = video.videoWidth;
   173→          canvas.height = video.videoHeight;
   174→          const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
   175→          if (ctx) {
   176→            ctx.drawImage(video, 0, 0);
   177→          }
   178→        }
   179→      }
   180→
   181→      // All frames captured
   182→      if (!cancelled) {
   183→        setCapturedForUrl(videoUrl);
   184→        setCapturedCount(3);
   185→        setIsExtracting(false);
   186→        if (urlChanged) {
   187→          onLoadComplete?.();
   188→        }
   189→      }
   190→    };
   191→
   192→    captureAllFrames().catch(err => {
   193→      if (!cancelled) {
   194→        normalizeAndPresentError(err, { context: 'StructureVideoPreview', showToast: false });
   195→        setIsExtracting(false);
   196→      }
   197→    });
   198→
   199→    return () => {
   200→      cancelled = true;
   201→    };
   202→  }, [videoUrl, framePositions, treatment, onLoadComplete]);
   203→
   204→  // Canvas visibility: show canvases once we have ANY captures for this URL
   205→  const hasCapturesForUrl = capturedForUrl === videoUrl && capturedCount >= 3;
   206→  const isFrameCaptured = (i: number) => capturedForUrl !== null && capturedCount > i;
   207→
   208→  return (
   209→    <div className="space-y-1.5">
   210→      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[10px]">
   211→        {hasCapturesForUrl || isExtracting ? (
   212→          <>
   213→            {isExtracting && !hasCapturesForUrl && (
   214→              <Loader2 className="w-3 h-3 animate-spin text-primary" />
   215→            )}
   216→            <span className="text-muted-foreground">
   217→              Frames {framePositions[0].frame} - {framePositions[2].frame} of structure video
   218→            </span>
   219→            <span className="text-primary/70 italic">Make changes on the timeline</span>
   220→          </>
   221→        ) : (
   222→          <>
   223→            <Loader2 className="w-3 h-3 animate-spin text-primary" />
   224→            <span className="text-muted-foreground">Loading video frames...</span>
   225→          </>
   226→        )}
   227→      </div>
   228→      <div className="flex gap-1">
   229→        {/* Hidden video for seeking */}
   230→        <video
   231→          ref={videoRef}
   232→          src={videoUrl}
   233→          className="hidden"
   234→          muted
   235→          playsInline
   236→          crossOrigin="anonymous"
   237→        />
   238→        {/* 3 frame previews */}
   239→        {[0, 1, 2].map((i) => (
   240→          <div key={i} className="flex-1 relative">
   241→            {/* Canvas always rendered (stable ref for drawing) - invisible until captured for current URL */}
   242→            <canvas
   243→              ref={el => { canvasRefs.current[i] = el; }}
   244→              className={`w-full aspect-video rounded object-cover ${!isFrameCaptured(i) ? 'invisible' : ''}`}
   245→            />
   246→            {/* Skeleton shown until frame is captured for current URL */}
   247→            {!isFrameCaptured(i) && (
   248→              <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-muted rounded animate-pulse" />
   249→            )}
   250→            <span className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 text-[8px] text-center bg-black/60 text-white py-0.5 rounded-b">
   251→              {framePositions[i].label}
   252→            </span>
   253→          </div>
   254→        ))}
   255→      </div>
   256→    </div>
   257→  );
   258→};
   259→
```

```
    68→

   429→                <p className={`${config.textSize} ${config.fontWeight}`}>{formatModelName(modelName)}</p>
   430→              </div>
   431→            )}
   432→            <div className="space-y-1">
   433→              <p className={`${config.textSize} font-medium text-muted-foreground`}>Resolution</p>
   434→              <p className={`${config.textSize} ${config.fontWeight}`}>{resolution || 'N/A'}</p>
   435→            </div>
   436→            <div className="space-y-1">
   437→              <p className={`${config.textSize} font-medium text-muted-foreground`}>{isSegmentTask ? 'Frames' : 'Frames / Segment'}</p>
   438→              <p className={`${config.textSize} ${config.fontWeight}`}>{frames || 'N/A'}</p>
   439→            </div>
   440→            {phaseConfig?.flow_shift !== undefined && (
   441→              <div className="space-y-1">
   442→                <p className={`${config.textSize} font-medium text-muted-foreground`}>Flow Shift</p>
   443→                <p className={`${config.textSize} ${config.fontWeight}`}>{phaseConfig.flow_shift}</p>
   444→              </div>
   445→            )}
   446→            {phaseConfig?.sample_solver && (
   447→              <div className="space-y-1">
   448→                <p className={`${config.textSize} font-medium text-muted-foreground`}>Solver</p>
   449→                <p className={`${config.textSize} ${config.fontWeight} capitalize`}>{phaseConfig.sample_solver}</p>
   450→              </div>
   451→            )}
   452→          </div>
   453→        )}
   454→
   455→        {!showPhaseContentInRightColumn && isAdvancedMode && renderPhaseConfigSection({ showSummary: false })}
   456→
   457→        {/* In basic mode OR no phases with loras: show "LoRAs" from additional_loras */}
   458→        {!showPhaseContentInRightColumn && (!isAdvancedMode || !phaseConfig?.phases?.length) && additionalLoras && Object.keys(additionalLoras).length > 0 && (
   459→          <div className="pt-2 border-t border-muted-foreground/20 space-y-2">
   460→            <p className={`${config.textSize} font-medium text-muted-foreground`}>LoRAs</p>
   461→            {Object.entries(additionalLoras).slice(0, config.maxLoras).map(([url, strength]) => (
   462→              <div key={url} className={`group/lora flex items-center gap-2 p-1.5 bg-background/50 rounded border ${config.textSize} min-w-0`}>
   463→                <span className={`${config.fontWeight} truncate min-w-0 flex-1`}>{getDisplayNameFromUrl(url, availableLoras)}</span>
   464→                <button
   465→                  onClick={() => { void handleCopyLoraUrl(url); }}
   466→                  className="p-0.5 rounded hover:bg-muted text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground transition-colors opacity-0 group-hover/lora:opacity-100 shrink-0"
   467→                  title="Copy LoRA URL"
   468→                >
   469→                  {copiedLoraUrl === url ? <Check className="w-3 h-3 text-green-500" /> : <Copy className="w-3 h-3" />}
   470→                </button>
   471→                <span className="text-muted-foreground shrink-0">{Number(strength).toFixed(1)}</span>
   472→              </div>
   473→            ))}
   474→          </div>
   475→        )}
   476→      </div>
   477→
   478→      {showPhaseContentInRightColumn && (
   479→        <div className="space-y-4 lg:border-l lg:border-muted-foreground/20 lg:pl-4 min-w-0">
   480→          {renderPhaseConfigSection({ showSummary: true, borderTop: 'pt-3' })}
   481→        </div>
   482→      )}
   483→    </div>
   484→  );
   485→};
   486→
```

```
1→/**
     2→ * VideoLightbox
     3→ *
     4→ * Specialized lightbox for video media. Handles all video-specific functionality:
     5→ * - Video trimming
     6→ * - Video replace mode
     7→ * - Video regenerate mode
     8→ * - Video enhance mode
     9→ * - Segment slot mode (timeline navigation)
    10→ *
    11→ * Uses useSharedLightboxState for shared functionality (variants, navigation, etc.)
    12→ *
    13→ * This is part of the split architecture where MediaLightbox dispatches to
    14→ * ImageLightbox or VideoLightbox based on media type.
    15→ */
    16→
    17→import React from 'react';
    18→import type { VideoLightboxProps, VideoLightboxPropsWithMedia } from './videoLightboxContracts';
    19→import { normalizeAndPresentError } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandling/runtimeError';

    22→import { LightboxProviders } from './components';

    38→
    39→export type {
    40→  VideoLightboxSharedStateModel,
    41→  VideoLightboxEditModel,
    42→} from './hooks/useVideoLightboxController';
    43→
    44→const VideoLightboxMediaContent: React.FC<VideoLightboxPropsWithMedia> = (props) => {
    45→  const modeModel = useVideoLightboxMode(props);
    46→  const env = useVideoLightboxEnvironment(props, modeModel);
    47→  const sharedState = useVideoLightboxSharedState(props, modeModel, env);
    48→  const editModel = useVideoLightboxEditing(props, modeModel, env, sharedState);
    49→  const renderModel = useVideoLightboxRenderModel(props, modeModel, env, sharedState, editModel);
    50→  const overlayViewport: OverlayViewportConstraints = {
    51→    tasksPaneOpen: env.effectiveTasksPaneOpen,
    52→    tasksPaneWidth: env.effectiveTasksPaneWidth,
    53→    tasksPaneLocked: env.isTasksPaneLocked,
    54→    isTabletOrLarger: sharedState.layout.isTabletOrLarger,
    55→    needsFullscreenLayout: renderModel.needsFullscreenLayout,
    56→    needsTasksPaneOffset: renderModel.needsTasksPaneOffset,
    57→  };
    58→
    59→  return (
    60→    <LightboxProviders stateValue={renderModel.lightboxStateValue}>
    61→      <VideoEditProvider value={editModel.videoEditValue}>
    62→        <LightboxShell
    63→          onClose={props.onClose}
    64→          hasCanvasOverlay={false}
    65→          isRepositionMode={false}
    66→          isMobile={env.isMobile}
    67→          overlayViewport={overlayViewport}
    68→          contentRef={env.contentRef}
    69→          accessibilityTitle={renderModel.accessibilityTitle}
    70→          accessibilityDescription={renderModel.accessibilityDescription}
    71→        >
    72→          {modeModel.isFormOnlyMode && props.segmentSlotMode ? (
    73→            <SegmentSlotFormView
    74→              segmentSlotMode={props.segmentSlotMode}
    75→              onClose={props.onClose}
    76→              onNavPrev={modeModel.handleSlotNavPrev}
    77→              onNavNext={modeModel.handleSlotNavNext}
    78→              hasPrevious={modeModel.hasPrevious}
    79→              hasNext={modeModel.hasNext}
    80→              readOnly={props.readOnly}
    81→            />
    82→          ) : (
    83→            <LightboxLayout {...renderModel.layoutProps} controlsPanelContent={renderModel.controlsPanelContent} />
    84→          )}
    85→        </LightboxShell>
    86→      </VideoEditProvider>
    87→    </LightboxProviders>
    88→  );
    89→};
    90→
    91→const VideoLightboxFormOnlyContent: React.FC<VideoLightboxProps> = (props) => {
    92→  const modeModel = useVideoLightboxMode(props);
    93→  const env = useVideoLightboxEnvironment(props, modeModel);
    94→
    95→  if (!props.segmentSlotMode) {
    96→    return null;
    97→  }
    98→
    99→  const overlayViewport: OverlayViewportConstraints = {
   100→    tasksPaneOpen: env.effectiveTasksPaneOpen,
   101→    tasksPaneWidth: env.effectiveTasksPaneWidth,
   102→    tasksPaneLocked: env.isTasksPaneLocked,
   103→    isTabletOrLarger: false,
   104→    needsFullscreenLayout: true,
   105→    needsTasksPaneOffset: false,
   106→  };
   107→
   108→  return (
   109→    <LightboxShell
   110→      onClose={props.onClose}
   111→      hasCanvasOverlay={false}
   112→      isRepositionMode={false}
   113→      isMobile={env.isMobile}
   114→      overlayViewport={overlayViewport}
   115→      contentRef={env.contentRef}
   116→      accessibilityTitle={`Segment ${(props.segmentSlotMode.currentIndex ?? 0) + 1} Settings`}
   117→      accessibilityDescription="Configure and generate this video segment. Use Tab or arrow keys to navigate between segments."
   118→    >
   119→      <SegmentSlotFormView
   120→        segmentSlotMode={props.segmentSlotMode}
   121→        onClose={props.onClose}
   122→        onNavPrev={modeModel.handleSlotNavPrev}
   123→        onNavNext={modeModel.handleSlotNavNext}
   124→        hasPrevious={modeModel.hasPrevious}
   125→        hasNext={modeModel.hasNext}
   126→        readOnly={props.readOnly}
   127→      />
   128→    </LightboxShell>
   129→  );
   130→};
   131→
   132→export const VideoLightbox: React.FC<VideoLightboxProps> = (props) => {
   133→  const isSegmentSlotMode = !!props.segmentSlotMode;
   134→  const hasSegmentVideo = isSegmentSlotMode && !!props.segmentSlotMode?.segmentVideo;
   135→  const isFormOnlyMode = isSegmentSlotMode && !hasSegmentVideo;
   136→
   137→  if (isFormOnlyMode) {
   138→    return <VideoLightboxFormOnlyContent {...props} />;
   139→  }
   140→
   141→  if (!props.media) {
   142→    normalizeAndPresentError(new Error('No media prop provided and not in form-only mode'), {
   143→      context: 'VideoLightbox',
   144→      showToast: false,
   145→    });
   146→    return null;
   147→  }
   148→
   149→  const media = props.parentGenerationIdOverride
   150→    ? {
   151→        ...props.media,
   152→        parent_generation_id: props.parentGenerationIdOverride,
   153→      }
   154→    : props.media;
   155→
   156→  return <VideoLightboxMediaContent {...props} media={media} />;
   157→};
   158→
```

```
   298→    effectiveTasksPaneOpen: env.effectiveTasksPaneOpen,
   299→    effectiveTasksPaneWidth: env.effectiveTasksPaneWidth,
   300→    workflowBar,
   301→    workflowControls: showPanel ? undefined : workflowControls,
   302→    buttonGroups: {
   303→      bottomLeft: sharedState.buttonGroupProps.bottomLeft,
   304→      bottomRight: sharedState.buttonGroupProps.bottomRight,
   305→      topRight: {
   306→        ...sharedState.buttonGroupProps.topRight,

   308→        handleDelete,
   309→      },
   310→    },
   311→    adjacentSegments,
   312→    segmentSlotMode: modeModel.hasSegmentVideo ? props.segmentSlotMode : undefined,
   313→  };
   314→
   315→  return {
   316→    lightboxStateValue,
   317→    controlsPanelContent,
   318→    layoutProps,
   319→    needsFullscreenLayout,
   320→    needsTasksPaneOffset,
   321→    accessibilityTitle,
   322→    accessibilityDescription,
   323→  };
   324→}
```

```
1→/**
     2→ * useVariants Hook
     3→ *
     4→ * Centralized hook for fetching and managing variants for a generation.
     5→ * Allows switching between variants and setting the primary variant.
     6→ * Supports realtime updates via RealtimeProvider.
     7→ *
     8→ * Used by: MediaLightbox, InlineEditView, and other components that display variants.
     9→ */
    10→
    11→import { useState, useCallback, useMemo, useEffect } from 'react';
    12→import { useQuery, useMutation, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
    13→import { getSupabaseClient as supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';
    14→import { invalidateVariantChange } from '@/shared/hooks/invalidation/useGenerationInvalidation';
    15→import { normalizeAndPresentError } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandling/runtimeError';
    16→import { generationQueryKeys } from '@/shared/lib/queryKeys/generations';
    17→import { useAppEventListener } from '@/shared/lib/typedEvents';
    18→
    19→/**
    20→ * A variant of a generation (from generation_variants table)
    21→ */
    22→export interface GenerationVariant {
    23→  id: string;
    24→  generation_id: string;
    25→  location: string;
    26→  thumbnail_url: string | null;
    27→  params: Record<string, unknown> | null;
    28→  is_primary: boolean;
    29→  starred: boolean;
    30→  variant_type: string | null;
    31→  name: string | null;
    32→  created_at: string;
    33→  viewed_at: string | null;
    34→}
    35→
    36→/**
    37→ * Return type for useVariants hook
    38→ */
    39→interface UseVariantsReturn {
    40→  variants: GenerationVariant[];
    41→  primaryVariant: GenerationVariant | null;
    42→  activeVariant: GenerationVariant | null;
    43→  isLoading: boolean;
    44→  error: Error | null;
    45→  refetch: () => void;
    46→  setActiveVariantId: (variantId: string | null) => void;
    47→  setPrimaryVariant: (variantId: string) => Promise<void>;
    48→  deleteVariant: (variantId: string) => Promise<void>;
    49→}
    50→
    51→interface UseVariantsProps {
    52→  generationId: string | null;
    53→  enabled?: boolean;
    54→}
    55→
    56→export const useVariants = ({
    57→  generationId,
    58→  enabled = true,
    59→}: UseVariantsProps): UseVariantsReturn => {
    60→  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
    61→  const [activeVariantId, setActiveVariantIdInternal] = useState<string | null>(null);
    62→  
    63→  // Stable callback - no deps needed since we just forward to internal setter
    64→  const setActiveVariantId = useCallback((variantId: string | null) => {
    65→    setActiveVariantIdInternal(variantId);
    66→  }, []);
    67→
    68→  // Fetch variants for this generation
    69→  const {
    70→    data: variants = [],
    71→    isLoading,
    72→    error,
    73→    refetch,
    74→  } = useQuery({
    75→    queryKey: generationId ? generationQueryKeys.variants(generationId) : ['generation-variants', null],
    76→    queryFn: async () => {
    77→      if (!generationId) return [];
    78→
    79→      const { data, error } = await supabase().from('generation_variants')
    80→        .select('*')
    81→        .eq('generation_id', generationId)
    82→        .order('created_at', { ascending: false });
    83→
    84→      if (error) {
    85→        console.error('[useVariants] Error fetching variants:', error);
    86→        throw error;
    87→      }
    88→
    89→      return (data || []) as GenerationVariant[];
    90→    },
    91→    enabled: enabled && !!generationId,
    92→    staleTime: 30000, // 30 seconds
    93→  });
    94→
    95→  // Listen for realtime variant changes and refetch when our generationId is affected
    96→  const handleVariantChange = useCallback((detail: { affectedGenerationIds: string[] }) => {
    97→    if (!generationId || !enabled) return;
    98→    const affectedIds = detail?.affectedGenerationIds || [];
    99→    if (affectedIds.includes(generationId)) {
   100→      refetch();
   101→    }
   102→  }, [generationId, enabled, refetch]);
   103→
   104→  useAppEventListener('realtime:variant-change-batch', handleVariantChange);
   105→
   106→  // Find the primary variant
   107→  const primaryVariant = useMemo(() => {
   108→    return variants.find((v) => v.is_primary) || null;
   109→  }, [variants]);
   110→
   111→  // Get the active variant (selected or primary, fallback to first variant)
   112→  const activeVariant = useMemo(() => {
   113→    if (activeVariantId) {
   114→      const found = variants.find((v) => v.id === activeVariantId);
   115→      if (found) {
   116→        return found;
   117→      }
   118→    }
   119→    // Fall back to primary, then first variant if no primary exists
   120→    return primaryVariant || variants[0] || null;
   121→  }, [variants, activeVariantId, primaryVariant]);
   122→
   123→  // Initialize active variant to primary (or first variant) when variants load
   124→  // NOTE: Using useEffect, not useMemo - side effects should not be in useMemo
   125→  useEffect(() => {
   126→    if (!activeVariantId && variants.length > 0) {
   127→      const variantToSelect = primaryVariant || variants[0];
   128→      if (variantToSelect) {
   129→        setActiveVariantIdInternal(variantToSelect.id);
   130→      }
   131→    }
   132→  }, [primaryVariant, variants, activeVariantId]);
   133→
   134→  // Mutation to set a variant as primary
   135→  const setPrimaryMutation = useMutation({
   136→    mutationFn: async (variantId: string) => {
   137→
   138→      // Update the variant to be primary
   139→      // The database trigger will handle unsetting the old primary
   140→      const { error } = await supabase().from('generation_variants')
   141→        .update({ is_primary: true })
   142→        .eq('id', variantId);
   143→
   144→      if (error) {
   145→        console.error('[useVariants] Error setting primary variant:', error);
   146→        throw error;
   147→      }
   148→
   149→      return variantId;
   150→    },
   151→    onSuccess: async (variantId) => {
   152→
   153→      // Invalidate caches using centralized function
   154→      if (generationId) {
   155→        await invalidateVariantChange(queryClient, {
   156→          generationId,
   157→          reason: 'set-primary-variant',
   158→        });
   159→      }
   160→
   161→      // SINGLE-SEGMENT PROPAGATION: If this is a child of a single-segment parent,
   162→      // also create a variant on the parent so the main generation updates
   163→      try {
   164→        // Get the promoted variant's location and thumbnail
   165→        const { data: promotedVariant } = await supabase().from('generation_variants')
   166→          .select('location, thumbnail_url, params')
   167→          .eq('id', variantId)
   168→          .single();
   169→
   170→        if (!promotedVariant || !generationId) return;
   171→
   172→        // Check if this generation is a child with a parent
   173→        const { data: generation } = await supabase().from('generations')
   174→          .select('is_child, parent_generation_id')
   175→          .eq('id', generationId)
   176→          .single();
   177→
   178→        if (!generation?.is_child || !generation?.parent_generation_id) return;
   179→
   180→        // Count how many children the parent has
   181→        const { count: childCount } = await supabase().from('generations')
   182→          .select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true })
   183→          .eq('parent_generation_id', generation.parent_generation_id)
   184→          .eq('is_child', true);
   185→
   186→        // Only propagate for single-segment parents
   187→        if (childCount !== 1) {
   188→          return;
   189→        }
   190→
   191→        // Create a new variant on the parent with the promoted video
   192→        // Exclude task-specific fields that shouldn't propagate to parent
   193→        const {
   194→          source_task_id: _sourceTaskId,
   195→          child_generation_id: _childGenId,
   196→          ...paramsToPropagate
   197→        } = (promotedVariant.params || {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
   198→
   199→        const { error: insertError } = await supabase().from('generation_variants')
   200→          .insert({
   201→            generation_id: generation.parent_generation_id,
   202→            location: promotedVariant.location,
   203→            thumbnail_url: promotedVariant.thumbnail_url,
   204→            is_primary: true, // DB trigger will unset old primary
   205→            variant_type: 'travel_segment',
   206→            params: {
   207→              ...paramsToPropagate,
   208→              propagated_from_child: generationId,
   209→              propagated_from_variant: variantId,
   210→            },
   211→          });
   212→
   213→        if (insertError) {
   214→          normalizeAndPresentError(insertError, { context: 'useVariants', showToast: false });
   215→        } else {
   216→          // Invalidate parent's variant cache
   217→          await invalidateVariantChange(queryClient, {
   218→            generationId: generation.parent_generation_id,
   219→            reason: 'single-segment-propagation',
   220→          });
   221→        }
   222→      } catch (err) {
   223→        console.error('[useVariants] Error in single-segment propagation:', err);
   224→        // Don't throw - this is a nice-to-have, not critical
   225→      }
   226→    },
   227→    onError: (error) => {
   228→      normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'useVariants.setPrimaryVariant', toastTitle: 'Failed to set primary variant' });
   229→    },
   230→  });
   231→
   232→  const setPrimaryVariant = useCallback(
   233→    async (variantId: string) => {
   234→      await setPrimaryMutation.mutateAsync(variantId);
   235→    },
   236→    [setPrimaryMutation]
   237→  );
   238→
   239→  // Mutation to delete a variant
   240→  const deleteVariantMutation = useMutation({
   241→    mutationFn: async (variantId: string) => {
   242→
   243→      // Check if variant is primary - don't allow deleting primary
   244→      const variant = variants.find(v => v.id === variantId);
   245→      if (variant?.is_primary) {
   246→        throw new Error('Cannot delete the primary variant');
   247→      }
   248→
   249→      const { error } = await supabase().from('generation_variants')
   250→        .delete()
   251→        .eq('id', variantId);
   252→
   253→      if (error) {
   254→        console.error('[useVariants] Error deleting variant:', error);
   255→        throw error;
   256→      }
   257→
   258→      return variantId;
   259→    },
   260→    onSuccess: async (variantId) => {
   261→
   262→      // If deleted variant was active, switch to primary
   263→      if (activeVariantId === variantId) {
   264→        setActiveVariantId(primaryVariant?.id || null);
   265→      }
   266→
   267→      // Invalidate caches
   268→      if (generationId) {
   269→        await invalidateVariantChange(queryClient, {
   270→          generationId,
   271→          reason: 'delete-variant',
   272→        });
   273→      }
   274→    },
   275→    onError: (error) => {
   276→      normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'useVariants.deleteVariant', toastTitle: 'Failed to delete variant' });
   277→    },
   278→  });
   279→
   280→  const deleteVariant = useCallback(
   281→    async (variantId: string) => {
   282→      await deleteVariantMutation.mutateAsync(variantId);
   283→    },
   284→    [deleteVariantMutation]
   285→  );
   286→
   287→  return {
   288→    variants,
   289→    primaryVariant,
   290→    activeVariant,
   291→    isLoading,
   292→    error: error as Error | null,
   293→    refetch,
   294→    setActiveVariantId,
   295→    setPrimaryVariant,
   296→    deleteVariant,
   297→  };
   298→};
   299→
```
