```
1→/**
     2→ * VideoEnhanceForm Component
     3→ *
     4→ * Form for video enhancement settings including:
     5→ * - Frame interpolation (FILM)
     6→ * - Video upscaling (FlashVSR)
     7→ *
     8→ * Toggle switches reveal settings for each enhancement type.
     9→ * Submit is disabled if neither mode is enabled.
    10→ */
    11→
    12→import React, { useEffect, useState, useRef } from 'react';
    13→import { Button } from '@/shared/components/ui/button';
    14→import { Label } from '@/shared/components/ui/primitives/label';
    15→import { Switch } from '@/shared/components/ui/switch';
    16→import { Slider } from '@/shared/components/ui/slider';
    17→import {
    18→  Select,
    19→  SelectContent,
    20→  SelectItem,
    21→  SelectTrigger,
    22→  SelectValue,
    23→} from '@/shared/components/ui/select';
    24→import { Loader2, Check, Sparkles, Film, ArrowUp } from 'lucide-react';
    25→import { cn } from '@/shared/components/ui/contracts/cn';
    26→import type { VideoEnhanceSettings } from '../hooks/useGenerationEditSettings';
    27→
    28→// Default FPS for AI-generated videos (matches useVideoEditing assumption)
    29→const DEFAULT_VIDEO_FPS = 16;
    30→
    31→/**
    32→ * Detect video FPS using requestVideoFrameCallback
    33→ * Returns null while detecting, then the detected FPS
    34→ */
    35→function useDetectVideoFps(videoUrl: string | undefined): number | null {
    36→  const [detectedFps, setDetectedFps] = useState<number | null>(null);
    37→  const videoRef = useRef<HTMLVideoElement | null>(null);
    38→
    39→  useEffect(() => {
    40→    if (!videoUrl) return;
    41→
    42→    // Create a hidden video element to detect FPS
    43→    const video = document.createElement('video');
    44→    video.src = videoUrl;
    45→    video.muted = true;
    46→    video.playsInline = true;
    47→    videoRef.current = video;
    48→
    49→    let frameCount = 0;
    50→    let startTime = 0;
    51→    let rafId: number | null = null;
    52→
    53→    const countFrame = (_now: number, metadata: VideoFrameCallbackMetadata) => {
    54→      if (frameCount === 0) {
    55→        // First frame - just record start time, don't count it
    56→        startTime = metadata.mediaTime;
    57→        frameCount++;
    58→        rafId = video.requestVideoFrameCallback(countFrame);
    59→        return;
    60→      }
    61→      frameCount++;
    62→
    63→      // After ~0.5 seconds of video time, calculate FPS
    64→      // frameCount-1 because we don't count the first frame (it's our reference point)
    65→      const elapsed = metadata.mediaTime - startTime;
    66→      if (elapsed >= 0.5 && frameCount > 2) {
    67→        const fps = Math.round((frameCount - 1) / elapsed);
    68→        setDetectedFps(fps);
    69→        video.pause();
    70→        return;
    71→      }
    72→
    73→      // Continue counting
    74→      rafId = video.requestVideoFrameCallback(countFrame);
    75→    };
    76→
    77→    video.addEventListener('loadedmetadata', () => {
    78→      // Start playing to count frames
    79→      if ('requestVideoFrameCallback' in video) {
    80→        rafId = video.requestVideoFrameCallback(countFrame);
    81→        video.play().catch(() => {
    82→          // If autoplay blocked, fall back to default
    83→          setDetectedFps(DEFAULT_VIDEO_FPS);
    84→        });
    85→      } else {
    86→        // Browser doesn't support requestVideoFrameCallback, use default
    87→        setDetectedFps(DEFAULT_VIDEO_FPS);
    88→      }
    89→    });
    90→
    91→    video.addEventListener('error', () => {
    92→      setDetectedFps(DEFAULT_VIDEO_FPS);
    93→    });
    94→
    95→    // Cleanup
    96→    return () => {
    97→      if (rafId !== null && videoRef.current) {
    98→        // Note: cancelVideoFrameCallback may not exist in all browsers
    99→        try {
   100→          (videoRef.current as HTMLVideoElement & { cancelVideoFrameCallback?: (id: number) => void }).cancelVideoFrameCallback?.(rafId);
   101→        } catch { /* intentionally ignored */ }
   102→      }
   103→      video.pause();
   104→      video.src = '';
   105→      videoRef.current = null;
   106→    };
   107→  }, [videoUrl]);
   108→
   109→  return detectedFps;
   110→}
   111→
   112→interface VideoEnhanceFormProps {
   113→  settings: VideoEnhanceSettings;
   114→  onUpdateSetting: <K extends keyof VideoEnhanceSettings>(
   115→    key: K,
   116→    value: VideoEnhanceSettings[K]
   117→  ) => void;
   118→  onGenerate: () => void;
   119→  isGenerating: boolean;
   120→  generateSuccess: boolean;
   121→  canSubmit: boolean;
   122→  variant?: 'desktop' | 'mobile';
   123→  /** Video URL for FPS detection */
   124→  videoUrl?: string;
   125→}
   126→
   127→export const VideoEnhanceForm: React.FC<VideoEnhanceFormProps> = ({
   128→  settings,
   129→  onUpdateSetting,
   130→  onGenerate,
   131→  isGenerating,
   132→  generateSuccess,
   133→  canSubmit,
   134→  variant = 'desktop',
   135→  videoUrl,
   136→}) => {
   137→  const isMobile = variant === 'mobile';
   138→
   139→  // Detect video FPS
   140→  const detectedFps = useDetectVideoFps(videoUrl);
   141→  const videoFps = detectedFps ?? DEFAULT_VIDEO_FPS;
   142→
   143→  // Calculate output FPS based on interpolation setting
   144→  // FILM adds num_frames between each pair, so output FPS ≈ input * (num_frames + 1)
   145→  const outputFps = Math.round(videoFps * (settings.numFrames + 1));
   146→
   147→  return (
   148→    <div className={cn("flex flex-col gap-4", isMobile ? "px-3 py-2" : "p-4")}>
   149→      {/* Interpolation Toggle & Settings */}
   150→      <div className="space-y-3">
```

```
1→/**
```

```
     9→
    10→import React, { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
    11→import { Check, Loader2, ArrowDown, ArrowUp, X, ImagePlus, Star } from 'lucide-react';

    25→import type { GenerationVariant } from '@/shared/hooks/useVariants';
    26→import type { RelationshipFilter } from './variantPresentation';
    27→import type { CurrentSegmentImagesData } from './variantSourceImages';

    31→
    32→/**
    33→ * Props for VariantSelector component
    34→ */
    35→interface VariantSelectorProps {
    36→  /** List of variants */
    37→  variants: GenerationVariant[];
    38→  /** Currently active variant ID */
    39→  activeVariantId: string | null;
    40→  /** Handler for variant selection */

    45→  isLoading?: boolean;

    48→  /** Whether a promotion is currently in progress */
    49→  isPromoting?: boolean;
    50→  /** Handler to load a variant's settings into the regenerate form */
    51→  onLoadVariantSettings?: (variantParams: Record<string, unknown>) => void;
    52→  /** Handler to delete a variant (not available for primary variant) */
    53→  onDeleteVariant?: (variantId: string) => Promise<void>;

    55→  readOnly?: boolean;
    56→  /** Handler to load a variant's source images onto the timeline */
    57→  onLoadVariantImages?: (variant: GenerationVariant) => void;
    58→  /** Current segment images data for comparison */
    59→  currentSegmentImages?: CurrentSegmentImagesData;
    60→}
    61→
    62→export const VariantSelector: React.FC<VariantSelectorProps> = ({
    63→  variants,
    64→  activeVariantId,
    65→  onVariantSelect,
    66→  onMakePrimary,
    67→  isLoading = false,
    68→  onPromoteToGeneration,
    69→  isPromoting = false,
    70→  onLoadVariantSettings,
    71→  onDeleteVariant,
    72→  readOnly = false,
    73→  onLoadVariantImages,
    74→  currentSegmentImages,
    75→}) => {
    76→  const [relationshipFilter, setRelationshipFilter] = useState<RelationshipFilter>('all');
    77→  const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState(0);
    78→  // Reset page when filter changes (prev-value ref avoids useEffect+setState)
    79→  const prevRelFilterRef = React.useRef(relationshipFilter);
    80→  if (prevRelFilterRef.current !== relationshipFilter) {
    81→    prevRelFilterRef.current = relationshipFilter;
    82→    if (currentPage !== 0) setCurrentPage(0);
    83→  }
    84→  const isMobile = useIsMobile();
    85→  const { data: availableLoras } = usePublicLoras();
    86→
    87→  const actions = useVariantActions({
    88→    variants,
    89→    activeVariantId,
    90→    isMobile,
    91→    onPromoteToGeneration,
    92→    onDeleteVariant,
    93→    onLoadVariantSettings,
    94→    onLoadVariantImages,
    95→  });
    96→
    97→  const starredCount = useMemo(() => variants.filter(v => v.starred).length, [variants]);
    98→
    99→  // Calculate variant relationships
   100→  const { parentVariants, childVariants, relationshipMap } = useMemo(() => {
   101→
   102→    const parents = new Set<string>();
   103→    const children = new Set<string>();
   104→    const relMap: Record<string, { isParent: boolean; isChild: boolean }> = {};
   105→
   106→    variants.forEach(variant => {
   107→      relMap[variant.id] = { isParent: false, isChild: false };
   108→    });
   109→
   110→    const activeVar = variants.find(variant => variant.id === activeVariantId);
   111→    if (!activeVar) {
   112→      return { parentVariants: parents, childVariants: children, relationshipMap: relMap };
   113→    }
   114→
   115→    const activeSourceVariantId = activeVar.params?.source_variant_id as string | undefined;
   116→
   117→    if (activeSourceVariantId) {
   118→      const parentVariant = variants.find(variant => variant.id === activeSourceVariantId);
   119→      if (parentVariant) {
   120→        parents.add(parentVariant.id);
   121→        relMap[parentVariant.id].isParent = true;
   122→      }
   123→    }
   124→
   125→    variants.forEach(variant => {
   126→      const sourceId = variant.params?.source_variant_id as string | undefined;
   127→      if (sourceId === activeVariantId) {
   128→        children.add(variant.id);
   129→        relMap[variant.id].isChild = true;
   130→      }
   131→    });
   132→
   133→    return { parentVariants: parents, childVariants: children, relationshipMap: relMap };
   134→  }, [variants, activeVariantId]);
   135→
   136→  // Sort variants with primary first
   137→  const sortedVariants = useMemo(() => {
   138→    return [...variants].sort((a, b) => {
   139→      if (a.is_primary && !b.is_primary) return -1;
   140→      if (!a.is_primary && b.is_primary) return 1;
   141→      return 0;
   142→    });
   143→  }, [variants]);
   144→
   145→  // Filter variants based on relationship filter
   146→  const filteredVariants = useMemo(() => {
   147→    if (relationshipFilter === 'all') return sortedVariants;
   148→    if (relationshipFilter === 'parents') {
   149→      return sortedVariants.filter(variant => parentVariants.has(variant.id) || variant.id === activeVariantId);
   150→    }
   151→    if (relationshipFilter === 'children') {
   152→      return sortedVariants.filter(variant => childVariants.has(variant.id) || variant.id === activeVariantId);
   153→    }
   154→    if (relationshipFilter === 'starred') {
   155→      return sortedVariants.filter(variant => variant.starred || variant.id === activeVariantId);
   156→    }
   157→    return sortedVariants;
   158→  }, [sortedVariants, relationshipFilter, parentVariants, childVariants, activeVariantId]);
   159→
   160→  const hasRelationships = parentVariants.size > 0 || childVariants.size > 0;
   161→  const showFilterRow = hasRelationships || starredCount > 0;
   162→
   163→  // Don't show if no variants at all
   164→  if (!isLoading && variants.length === 0) {
   165→    return null;
   166→  }
   167→
   168→  // Find the selected variant for the mobile info modal
   169→  const mobileInfoVariant = actions.mobileInfoVariantId
   170→    ? variants.find(variant => variant.id === actions.mobileInfoVariantId)
   171→    : null;
   172→
   173→  if (isLoading) {
   174→    return (
   175→      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 p-2 bg-background/80 backdrop-blur-sm rounded-lg">
   176→        <Skeleton className="w-16 h-10 rounded" />
   177→      </div>
   178→    );
   179→  }
   180→
   181→  return (
   182→    <>
   183→    <TooltipProvider delayDuration={300}>
   184→      <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 p-2 bg-background/90 backdrop-blur-sm rounded-lg border border-border/50 shadow-lg overflow-hidden">
   185→        {/* Header section */}
   186→        <div className={cn("flex gap-2", isMobile ? "flex-col" : "items-center justify-between")}>
   187→          {/* Row 1: Label + Action buttons */}
   188→          <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
   189→            <span className="text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground">Variants ({variants.length})</span>
   190→            {!readOnly && (
   191→            <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
   192→              {onPromoteToGeneration && (
   193→                <Tooltip>
   194→                  <TooltipTrigger asChild>
   195→                    <Button
   196→                      size="sm"
   197→                      variant="outline"
   198→                      onClick={actions.handlePromoteToGeneration}
   199→                      disabled={actions.localIsPromoting || isPromoting}
   200→                      className={cn(
   201→                        "h-auto min-h-6 text-xs px-2 py-1 gap-1 whitespace-normal text-left",
   202→                        actions.promoteSuccess && "bg-green-500/20 border-green-500/50 text-green-400"
   203→                      )}
   204→                    >
   205→                      {actions.localIsPromoting || isPromoting ? (
   206→                        <Loader2 className="w-3 h-3 animate-spin shrink-0" />
   207→                      ) : actions.promoteSuccess ? (
   208→                        <Check className="w-3 h-3 shrink-0" />
   209→                      ) : (
   210→                        <ImagePlus className="w-3 h-3 shrink-0" />
   211→                      )}
   212→                      {actions.promoteSuccess ? 'Created!' : 'New image'}
   213→                    </Button>

   216→                    <p>Create a standalone image from this variant</p>
   217→                  </TooltipContent>
   218→                </Tooltip>
   219→              )}
   220→            </div>
   221→            )}
   222→          </div>
   223→
   224→          {/* Filter buttons (relationships + starred) */}
   225→          {showFilterRow && (
   226→            <div className="flex items-center gap-1 justify-start">
   227→              {hasRelationships && (
   228→                <>
   229→                  <Tooltip>
   230→                    <TooltipTrigger asChild>
   231→                      <button
   232→                        onClick={() => setRelationshipFilter(relationshipFilter === 'parents' ? 'all' : 'parents')}
   233→                        className={cn(
   234→                          'flex items-center gap-0.5 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] transition-colors',
   235→                          relationshipFilter === 'parents'
   236→                            ? 'bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-400 border border-blue-500/50'
   237→                            : 'bg-muted/50 text-muted-foreground hover:bg-muted',
   238→                          parentVariants.size === 0 && 'opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed'
   239→                        )}
   240→                        disabled={parentVariants.size === 0}
   241→                      >
   242→                        <ArrowUp className="w-2.5 h-2.5" />
   243→                        <span>Based on ({parentVariants.size})</span>
   244→                      </button>
   245→                    </TooltipTrigger>
   246→                    <TooltipContent side="top" className="z-[100001]">
   247→                      <p>Show variants this is based on</p>
   248→                    </TooltipContent>
   249→                  </Tooltip>
   250→
   251→                  <Tooltip>
   252→                    <TooltipTrigger asChild>
   253→                      <button
   254→                        onClick={() => setRelationshipFilter(relationshipFilter === 'children' ? 'all' : 'children')}
   255→                        className={cn(
   256→                          'flex items-center gap-0.5 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] transition-colors',
   257→                          relationshipFilter === 'children'
   258→                            ? 'bg-purple-500/20 text-purple-400 border border-purple-500/50'
   259→                            : 'bg-muted/50 text-muted-foreground hover:bg-muted',
   260→                          childVariants.size === 0 && 'opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed'
   261→                        )}
   262→                        disabled={childVariants.size === 0}
   263→                      >
   264→                        <ArrowDown className="w-2.5 h-2.5" />
   265→                        <span>Based on this ({childVariants.size})</span>
   266→                      </button>
   267→                    </TooltipTrigger>
   268→                    <TooltipContent side="top" className="z-[100001]">
   269→                      <p>Show variants based on this one</p>
   270→                    </TooltipContent>
   271→                  </Tooltip>
   272→                </>
   273→              )}
   274→
   275→              {starredCount > 0 && (
   276→                <Tooltip>
   277→                  <TooltipTrigger asChild>
   278→                    <button
   279→                      onClick={() => setRelationshipFilter(relationshipFilter === 'starred' ? 'all' : 'starred')}
   280→                      className={cn(
   281→                        'flex items-center gap-0.5 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] transition-colors',
   282→                        relationshipFilter === 'starred'
   283→                          ? 'bg-yellow-500/20 text-yellow-400 border border-yellow-500/50'
   284→                          : 'bg-muted/50 text-muted-foreground hover:bg-muted'
   285→                      )}
   286→                    >
   287→                      <Star className={cn('w-2.5 h-2.5', relationshipFilter === 'starred' && 'fill-current')} />
   288→                      <span>Starred ({starredCount})</span>
   289→                    </button>
   290→                  </TooltipTrigger>
   291→                  <TooltipContent side="top" className="z-[100001]">
   292→                    <p>Show starred variants</p>
   293→                  </TooltipContent>
   294→                </Tooltip>
   295→              )}
   296→
   297→              {relationshipFilter !== 'all' && (
   298→                <button
   299→                  onClick={() => setRelationshipFilter('all')}
   300→                  className="p-0.5 rounded hover:bg-muted text-muted-foreground"
   301→                >
   302→                  <X className="w-3 h-3" />
   303→                </button>
   304→              )}
   305→            </div>
   306→          )}
   307→        </div>
   308→
   309→        {/* Grid with pagination */}
   310→        <VariantGrid
   311→          filteredVariants={filteredVariants}
   312→          allVariants={variants}
   313→          activeVariantId={activeVariantId}
   314→          currentPage={currentPage}
   315→          onPageChange={setCurrentPage}
   316→          isMobile={isMobile}
   317→          readOnly={readOnly}
   318→          availableLoras={availableLoras}
   319→          relationshipMap={relationshipMap}
   320→          variantLineageDepth={actions.variantLineageDepth}
   321→          copiedVariantId={actions.copiedVariantId}
   322→          loadedSettingsVariantId={actions.loadedSettingsVariantId}
   323→          onVariantSelect={onVariantSelect}
   324→          onMakePrimary={onMakePrimary}
   325→          onDeleteVariant={onDeleteVariant ? actions.handleDeleteVariant : undefined}
   326→          onLoadVariantSettings={onLoadVariantSettings}
   327→          onToggleStar={actions.handleToggleStar}
   328→          onMouseEnter={actions.handleVariantMouseEnter}
   329→          onShowMobileInfo={actions.setMobileInfoVariantId}
   330→          onShowLineageGif={actions.setLineageGifVariantId}
   331→          onCopyId={actions.handleCopyId}
   332→          onLoadSettings={actions.handleLoadSettings}
   333→          onLoadImages={onLoadVariantImages ? actions.handleLoadImages : undefined}
   334→          currentSegmentImages={currentSegmentImages}
   335→          loadedImagesVariantId={actions.loadedImagesVariantId}
   336→          isDeleteLoading={actions.isDeleteLoading}
   337→        />
   338→      </div>
   339→    </TooltipProvider>
   340→
   341→    {/* Lineage GIF Modal - lazy loaded since only opened on demand */}
   342→    <ChunkLoadErrorBoundary>
   343→      <React.Suspense fallback={null}>
   344→        <LazyLineageGifModal
   345→          open={!!actions.lineageGifVariantId}
   346→          onClose={() => actions.setLineageGifVariantId(null)}
   347→          variantId={actions.lineageGifVariantId}
   348→        />
   349→      </React.Suspense>
   350→    </ChunkLoadErrorBoundary>
   351→
   352→    {/* Mobile variant info modal */}
   353→    {isMobile && mobileInfoVariant && (
   354→      <MobileInfoModal
   355→        variant={mobileInfoVariant}
   356→        activeVariantId={activeVariantId}
   357→        availableLoras={availableLoras}
   358→        readOnly={readOnly}
   359→        onClose={() => actions.setMobileInfoVariantId(null)}
   360→        onMakePrimary={onMakePrimary}
   361→        onLoadVariantSettings={onLoadVariantSettings}
   362→        onLoadImages={onLoadVariantImages ? actions.handleLoadImages : undefined}
   363→        currentSegmentImages={currentSegmentImages}
   364→        loadedImagesVariantId={actions.loadedImagesVariantId}
   365→      />
   366→    )}
   367→    </>
   368→  );
   369→};
   370→
```

tool_result Read
```
1→/**
     2→ * useEffectiveMedia - Computes effective URLs and dimensions for media display
     3→ *
     4→ * Handles:
     5→ * - effectiveVideoUrl: Video URL respecting active variant
     6→ * - effectiveMediaUrl: Image URL respecting active variant
     7→ * - effectiveImageDimensions: Guaranteed dimensions with fallbacks
     8→ */
     9→
    10→import { useMemo } from 'react';
    11→import { ASPECT_RATIO_TO_RESOLUTION } from '@/shared/lib/media/aspectRatios';
    12→
    13→interface UseEffectiveMediaProps {
    14→  isVideo: boolean;
    15→  activeVariant: {
    16→    id?: string | null;
    17→    location?: string | null;
    18→    thumbnail_url?: string | null;
    19→    is_primary?: boolean | null;
    20→  } | null;
    21→  effectiveImageUrl: string | undefined;
    22→  imageDimensions: { width: number; height: number } | null;
    23→  projectAspectRatio: string | undefined;
    24→}
    25→
    26→interface UseEffectiveMediaReturn {
    27→  effectiveVideoUrl: string | undefined;
    28→  effectiveMediaUrl: string | undefined;
    29→  effectiveImageDimensions: { width: number; height: number };
    30→}
    31→
    32→export function useEffectiveMedia({
    33→  isVideo,
    34→  activeVariant,
    35→  effectiveImageUrl,
    36→  imageDimensions,
    37→  projectAspectRatio,
    38→}: UseEffectiveMediaProps): UseEffectiveMediaReturn {
    39→  // Get the effective video URL (active variant or current media)
    40→  const effectiveVideoUrl = useMemo(() => {
    41→    if (isVideo && activeVariant) {
    42→      return activeVariant.location ?? undefined;
    43→    }
    44→    return effectiveImageUrl;
    45→  }, [isVideo, activeVariant, effectiveImageUrl]);
    46→
    47→  // For images, use the active variant's location when a variant is explicitly selected
    48→  const effectiveMediaUrl = useMemo(() => {
    49→
    50→    // If an active variant is set (any variant, including primary), use its location
    51→    if (activeVariant && activeVariant.location) {
    52→      return activeVariant.location;
    53→    }
    54→    // Otherwise use the standard effective image URL
    55→    return effectiveImageUrl;
    56→  }, [activeVariant, effectiveImageUrl]);
    57→
    58→  // Compute effective dimensions that are GUARANTEED to have a value
    59→  // This is computed synchronously during render, so there's no flicker
    60→  // Priority: extracted/loaded dimensions > project aspect ratio > 16:9 default
    61→  const effectiveImageDimensions = useMemo(() => {
    62→    // Use actual dimensions if we have them
    63→    if (imageDimensions) {
    64→      return imageDimensions;
    65→    }
    66→
    67→    // Fallback to project aspect ratio
    68→    if (projectAspectRatio) {
    69→      const resolution = ASPECT_RATIO_TO_RESOLUTION[projectAspectRatio];
    70→      if (resolution && resolution.includes('x')) {
    71→        const [w, h] = resolution.split('x').map(Number);
    72→        if (!isNaN(w) && !isNaN(h) && w > 0 && h > 0) {
    73→          return { width: w, height: h };
    74→        }
    75→      }
    76→    }
    77→
    78→    // Absolute last resort: 16:9 default
    79→    return { width: 1920, height: 1080 };
    80→  }, [imageDimensions, projectAspectRatio]);
    81→
    82→  return {
    83→    effectiveVideoUrl,
    84→    effectiveMediaUrl,
    85→    effectiveImageDimensions,
    86→  };
    87→}
    88→
```

```
    16→} from '../../contexts/LightboxStateContext';
    17→import { useImageEditCanvasSafe } from '../../contexts/ImageEditCanvasContext';
    18→import { useVideoEditSafe } from '../../contexts/VideoEditContext';
    19→import { VariantOverlayBadge } from './VariantOverlayBadge';
    20→import { NewImageOverlayButton } from './NewImageOverlayButton';
    21→import { AdjacentSegmentNavigation } from './AdjacentSegmentNavigation';
    22→import { PreviewSequencePill } from './PreviewSequencePill';
    23→import { ConstituentImageNavigation } from './ConstituentImageNavigation';
    24→import { NavigationArrows } from '../NavigationArrows';
    25→import { FloatingToolControls } from '../FloatingToolControls';
    26→import { AnnotationFloatingControls } from '../AnnotationFloatingControls';
    27→import {
    28→  TopRightControls,
    29→  BottomLeftControls,
    30→  BottomRightControls,
    31→} from '../ButtonGroups';
    32→import { MediaDisplayWithCanvas } from '../MediaDisplayWithCanvas';
    33→import { VideoEditModeDisplay } from '../VideoEditModeDisplay';
    34→import { VideoTrimModeDisplay } from '../VideoTrimModeDisplay';
    35→import { WorkflowControls } from '../WorkflowControls';
    36→import { WorkflowControlsBar } from '../WorkflowControlsBar';
    37→
    38→function useLightboxLayoutModel(props: LightboxLayoutProps) {
    39→  const core = useLightboxCoreSafe();
    40→  const mediaState = useLightboxMediaSafe();
    41→  const variantsState = useLightboxVariantsSafe();
    42→  const navigation = useLightboxNavigationSafe();
    43→  const imageEdit = useImageEditCanvasSafe();
    44→  const videoEdit = useVideoEditSafe();
    45→
    46→  const isSidePanelLayout = props.showPanel && props.shouldShowSidePanel;
    47→  const isStackedLayout = props.showPanel && !props.shouldShowSidePanel;
    48→  const floatingToolVariant: 'mobile' | 'tablet' = core.isMobile ? 'mobile' : 'tablet';
    49→  const mediaDisplayVariant: 'desktop-side-panel' | 'mobile-stacked' | 'regular-centered' = props.showPanel
    50→    ? (isSidePanelLayout ? 'desktop-side-panel' : 'mobile-stacked')
    51→    : 'regular-centered';
    52→  const mediaDisplayContainerClassName = isSidePanelLayout
    53→    ? 'max-w-full max-h-full'
    54→    : 'w-full h-full';
    55→  const mediaDisplayDebugContext = props.showPanel
    56→    ? (isSidePanelLayout ? 'Desktop' : 'Mobile Stacked')
    57→    : 'Regular Centered';
    58→  const topCenterClassName = isStackedLayout
    59→    ? 'absolute top-4 md:top-16 left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 z-[60] flex flex-col items-center gap-2'
    60→    : 'absolute top-4 left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 z-[60] flex flex-col items-center gap-2';
    61→
    62→  return {
    63→    props,
    64→    core,
    65→    mediaState,
    66→    variantsState,
    67→    navigation,
    68→    imageEdit,
    69→    videoEdit,
    70→    isSidePanelLayout,
    71→    isStackedLayout,
    72→    floatingToolVariant,
    73→    mediaDisplayVariant,
    74→    mediaDisplayContainerClassName,
    75→    mediaDisplayDebugContext,
    76→    topCenterClassName,
    77→  };
    78→}
    79→
    80→type LightboxLayoutModel = ReturnType<typeof useLightboxLayoutModel>;
    81→
    82→function MediaContent({ model }: { model: LightboxLayoutModel }) {
    83→  const { mediaState, variantsState, videoEdit } = model;
    84→
    85→  if (mediaState.isVideo && videoEdit.isVideoEditModeActive && videoEdit.videoEditing) {
    86→    return (
    87→      <VideoEditModeDisplay
    88→        videoRef={videoEdit.videoEditing.videoRef}
    89→        videoUrl={mediaState.effectiveVideoUrl}
    90→        posterUrl={variantsState.activeVariant?.thumbnail_url || mediaState.media.thumbUrl}
    91→        videoDuration={videoEdit.videoDuration}
    92→        onLoadedMetadata={videoEdit.setVideoDuration}
    93→        selections={videoEdit.videoEditing.selections}
    94→        activeSelectionId={videoEdit.videoEditing.activeSelectionId}
    95→        onSelectionChange={videoEdit.videoEditing.handleUpdateSelection}
    96→        onSelectionClick={videoEdit.videoEditing.setActiveSelectionId}
    97→        onRemoveSelection={videoEdit.videoEditing.handleRemoveSelection}
    98→        onAddSelection={videoEdit.videoEditing.handleAddSelection}
    99→      />
   100→    );
```

```
1→/**
     2→ * InfoPanel Component
     3→ *
     4→ * Unified info panel for both desktop and mobile layouts.
     5→ * Shows task details, variants, and Info/Edit toggle controls.
     6→ *
     7→ * Uses context hooks for shared state (core, media, edit, variants).
     8→ * Receives only layout-specific and deeply-nested props.
     9→ */
    10→
    11→import React from 'react';
    12→import { Button } from '@/shared/components/ui/button';
    13→import { SegmentedControl, SegmentedControlItem } from '@/shared/components/ui/segmented-control';
    14→import { X, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
    15→import { cn } from '@/shared/components/ui/contracts/cn';
    16→
    17→import { TaskDetailsPanelWrapper } from './TaskDetailsPanelWrapper';
    18→import { VariantSelector } from '@/shared/components/VariantSelector';
    19→import { VariantBadge } from '@/shared/components/VariantBadge';
    20→import {
    21→  useLightboxCoreSafe,
    22→  useLightboxMediaSafe,
    23→  useLightboxVariantsSafe,
    24→} from '../contexts/LightboxStateContext';
    25→import { useImageEditCanvasSafe } from '../contexts/ImageEditCanvasContext';
    26→import { useVideoEditSafe } from '../contexts/VideoEditContext';
    27→import type { GenerationRow } from '@/domains/generation/types';
    28→import type { TaskDetailsData } from '../types';
    29→import { useCopyToClipboard } from '@/shared/hooks/clipboard/useCopyToClipboard';
    30→import type { DerivedItem } from '@/domains/generation/hooks/useDerivedItems';
    31→
    32→interface InfoPanelProps {
    33→  /** Layout variant */
    34→  variant: 'desktop' | 'mobile';
    35→
    36→  // Header toggle - only specialized props now
    37→  showImageEditTools: boolean;
    38→
    39→  // TaskDetailsPanelWrapper props (deeply nested data)
    40→  taskDetailsData: TaskDetailsData | undefined;
    41→  derivedItems: DerivedItem[];
    42→  derivedGenerations: GenerationRow[] | null;
    43→  paginatedDerived: DerivedItem[];
    44→  derivedPage: number;
    45→  derivedTotalPages: number;
    46→  onSetDerivedPage: (page: number) => void;
    47→  onNavigateToGeneration?: (generationId: string, derivedContext?: string[]) => Promise<void>;
    48→  currentMediaId: string;
    49→  currentShotId?: string;
    50→  replaceImages: boolean;
    51→  onReplaceImagesChange: (value: boolean) => void;
    52→  onSwitchToPrimary?: () => void;
    53→
    54→  /** Task ID for copy functionality (fallback when not in taskDetailsData) */
    55→  taskId?: string | null;
    56→}
    57→
    58→export const InfoPanel: React.FC<InfoPanelProps> = ({
    59→  variant,
    60→  // Header props
    61→  showImageEditTools,
    62→  // TaskDetails props
    63→  taskDetailsData,
    64→  derivedItems,
    65→  derivedGenerations,
    66→  paginatedDerived,
    67→  derivedPage,
    68→  derivedTotalPages,
    69→  onSetDerivedPage,
    70→  onNavigateToGeneration,
    71→  currentMediaId,
    72→  currentShotId,
    73→  replaceImages,
    74→  onReplaceImagesChange,
    75→  onSwitchToPrimary,
    76→  // Task ID fallback
    77→  taskId: taskIdProp,
    78→}) => {
    79→  const isMobile = variant === 'mobile';
    80→
    81→  // ========================================
    82→  // CONTEXT STATE (no longer from props)
    83→  // ========================================
    84→  const { onClose, readOnly } = useLightboxCoreSafe();
    85→  const { isVideo } = useLightboxMediaSafe();
    86→  const { isInpaintMode, handleEnterInpaintMode, handleExitInpaintMode } = useImageEditCanvasSafe();
    87→  const { isInVideoEditMode, handleEnterVideoEditMode, handleExitVideoEditMode } = useVideoEditSafe();
    88→  const {
    89→    variants,
    90→    activeVariant,
    91→    primaryVariant,
    92→    handleVariantSelect: onVariantSelect,
    93→    handleMakePrimary: onMakePrimary,
    94→    isLoadingVariants,
    95→    variantsSectionRef,
    96→    pendingTaskCount,
    97→    unviewedVariantCount,
    98→    onMarkAllViewed,
    99→    handlePromoteToGeneration: onPromoteToGeneration,
   100→    isPromoting,
   101→    handleDeleteVariant: onDeleteVariant,
   102→    onLoadVariantSettings,
   103→    onLoadVariantImages,
   104→    currentSegmentImages,
   105→  } = useLightboxVariantsSafe();
   106→
   107→  const hasVariants = variants && variants.length >= 1;
   108→
   109→  // Get task ID for copy functionality - use prop as fallback for videos
   110→  const taskId = taskDetailsData?.taskId || taskIdProp;
   111→  const { copied: idCopied, handleCopy: handleCopyId } = useCopyToClipboard(taskId ?? undefined);
   112→
   113→  // Render the Info/Edit toggle (shared for image and video modes)
   114→  const renderEditToggle = () => {
   115→    // Determine which edit mode applies
   116→    const isImageEdit = showImageEditTools && !isVideo;
   117→    const isVideoEdit = isVideo;
   118→
   119→    if (readOnly || (!isImageEdit && !isVideoEdit)) return null;
   120→
   121→    const isActive = isImageEdit ? isInpaintMode : isInVideoEditMode;
   122→    const handleEnter = isImageEdit ? handleEnterInpaintMode : handleEnterVideoEditMode;
   123→    const handleExit = isImageEdit ? handleExitInpaintMode : handleExitVideoEditMode;
   124→
   125→    return (
   126→      <SegmentedControl
   127→        value={isActive ? 'edit' : 'info'}
   128→        onValueChange={(value) => {
   129→          if (value === 'info' && isActive) {
   130→            handleExit();
   131→          } else if (value === 'edit' && !isActive) {
   132→            handleEnter();
   133→          }
   134→        }}
   135→      >
   136→        <SegmentedControlItem value="info">Info</SegmentedControlItem>
   137→        <SegmentedControlItem value="edit">Edit</SegmentedControlItem>
   138→      </SegmentedControl>
   139→    );
   140→  };
   141→
   142→  // Render the header - consistent single row layout for both mobile and desktop
   143→  const renderHeader = () => (
   144→    <div className={cn(
   145→      "flex-shrink-0 border-b border-border bg-background",
   146→      isMobile ? "sticky top-0 z-[80] px-3 py-2" : "p-4"
   147→    )}>
   148→      {/* Single row: ID + variants on left, toggle + close on right */}
   149→      <div className={cn(
   150→        "flex items-center justify-between",
   151→        isMobile ? "gap-2" : "gap-3"
   152→      )}>
   153→        {/* Left side - copy id + variants link */}
   154→        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
   155→          {taskId && (
   156→            <button
   157→              onClick={handleCopyId}
   158→              className={cn(
   159→                "px-2 py-1 text-xs rounded transition-colors touch-manipulation",
   160→                idCopied
   161→                  ? "text-green-400 bg-green-400/10"
   162→                  : "text-zinc-500 hover:text-zinc-200 hover:bg-zinc-700"
   163→              )}
   164→            >
   165→              {idCopied ? 'copied' : 'id'}
   166→            </button>
   167→          )}
   168→          {hasVariants && (
   169→            <button
   170→              onClick={() => variantsSectionRef?.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' })}
   171→              className="flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-1 text-xs rounded bg-muted/50 text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground hover:bg-muted transition-colors touch-manipulation"
   172→            >
   173→              <span>{variants.length} variants</span>
   174→              <svg className="w-2.5 h-2.5" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor">
   175→                <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M19 14l-7 7m0 0l-7-7m7 7V3" />
   176→              </svg>
   177→            </button>
   178→          )}
   179→          {pendingTaskCount > 0 ? (
   180→            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2 py-1 text-xs rounded bg-primary/10 text-primary">
   181→              <Loader2 className="w-3 h-3 animate-spin" />
   182→              <span>{pendingTaskCount} pending</span>
   183→            </div>
   184→          ) : variants && variants.length > 1 && unviewedVariantCount > 0 ? (
   185→            <VariantBadge
   186→              variant="inline"
   187→              derivedCount={variants.length}
   188→              unviewedVariantCount={unviewedVariantCount}
   189→              hasUnviewedVariants={true}
   190→              tooltipSide="bottom"
   191→              onMarkAllViewed={onMarkAllViewed}
   192→              onClick={() => variantsSectionRef?.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' })}
   193→            />
   194→          ) : null}
   195→        </div>
   196→
   197→        {/* Right side - toggles and close button */}
   198→        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
   199→          {renderEditToggle()}
   200→          <Button
   201→            variant="ghost"
   202→            size="sm"
   203→            onClick={onClose}
   204→            className={cn("p-0 hover:bg-muted", isMobile ? "h-7 w-7" : "h-8 w-8")}
   205→          >
   206→            <X className={cn(isMobile ? "h-3.5 w-3.5" : "h-4 w-4")} />
   207→          </Button>
   208→        </div>
   209→      </div>
   210→    </div>
   211→  );
   212→
   213→  // Render task details wrapper
   214→  const renderTaskDetails = () => (
   215→    <TaskDetailsPanelWrapper
   216→      taskDetailsData={taskDetailsData}
   217→      derivedItems={derivedItems}
   218→      derivedGenerations={derivedGenerations}
   219→      paginatedDerived={paginatedDerived}
   220→      derivedPage={derivedPage}
   221→      derivedTotalPages={derivedTotalPages}
   222→      onSetDerivedPage={onSetDerivedPage}
   223→      onNavigateToGeneration={onNavigateToGeneration}
   224→      onVariantSelect={onVariantSelect}
   225→      currentMediaId={currentMediaId}
   226→      currentShotId={currentShotId}
   227→      replaceImages={replaceImages}
   228→      onReplaceImagesChange={onReplaceImagesChange}
   229→      onClose={onClose}
   230→      variant={variant}
   231→      activeVariant={activeVariant}
   232→      primaryVariant={primaryVariant}
   233→      onSwitchToPrimary={onSwitchToPrimary}
   234→    />
   235→  );
   236→
   237→  // Render variants section - matches EditPanelLayout styling
   238→  const renderVariants = () => {
   239→    if (!hasVariants) return null;
   240→
   241→    // Match EditPanelLayout: border-t, consistent padding
   242→    const variantPadding = isMobile ? 'pt-2 mt-2 px-3 pb-2' : 'pt-4 mt-4 p-6';
   243→
   244→    return (
   245→      <div
   246→        ref={variantsSectionRef}
   247→        className={cn("border-t border-border", variantPadding)}
   248→      >
   249→        <VariantSelector
   250→          variants={variants}
   251→          activeVariantId={activeVariant?.id || null}
   252→          onVariantSelect={onVariantSelect}
   253→          onMakePrimary={onMakePrimary}
   254→          isLoading={isLoadingVariants}
   255→          onPromoteToGeneration={onPromoteToGeneration}
   256→          isPromoting={isPromoting}
   257→          onDeleteVariant={onDeleteVariant}
   258→          onLoadVariantSettings={onLoadVariantSettings}
   259→          onLoadVariantImages={onLoadVariantImages}
   260→          currentSegmentImages={currentSegmentImages}
   261→          readOnly={readOnly}
   262→        />
   263→      </div>
   264→    );
   265→  };
   266→
   267→  // Both desktop and mobile: variants inside scroll area (matches EditPanelLayout)
   268→  return (
   269→    <div className={cn("w-full flex flex-col", !isMobile && "h-full")}>
   270→      {renderHeader()}
   271→
   272→      {/* Scrollable content area - contains both task details and variants */}
   273→      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto min-h-0 overscroll-none">
   274→        {renderTaskDetails()}
   275→        {renderVariants()}
   276→      </div>
   277→    </div>
   278→  );
   279→};
   280→
```

```
1→/**
     2→ * EditPanelLayout Component
     3→ *
     4→ * Shared layout for edit panels (images and videos).
     5→ * Provides consistent header, mode selector, scrollable content area, and variants section.
     6→ */
     7→
     8→import React, { useRef } from 'react';
     9→import { Button } from '@/shared/components/ui/button';
    10→import { X, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
    11→import { cn } from '@/shared/components/ui/contracts/cn';
    12→import { SegmentedControl, SegmentedControlItem } from '@/shared/components/ui/segmented-control';
    13→import { VariantBadge } from '@/shared/components/VariantBadge';
    14→import { useLightboxVariantsSafe } from '../contexts/LightboxStateContext';
    15→import { VariantSelector } from '@/shared/components/VariantSelector';
    16→import type { GenerationVariant } from '@/shared/hooks/useVariants';
    17→import { useCopyToClipboard } from '@/shared/hooks/clipboard/useCopyToClipboard';
    18→
    19→interface EditPanelLayoutProps {
    20→  /** Layout variant */
    21→  variant: 'desktop' | 'mobile';
    22→
    23→  /** Handler to close the lightbox */
    24→  onClose: () => void;
    25→
    26→  /** Handler to exit edit mode (switch to info view) */
    27→  onExitEditMode: () => void;
    28→
    29→  /** Whether to hide the Info/Edit toggle */
    30→  hideInfoEditToggle?: boolean;
    31→
    32→  /**
    33→   * Simplified header mode for page views (edit-images, edit-video tools).
    34→   * When true, shows only [ModeSelector | X button] in header.
    35→   * When false (default), shows full header with id copy, variants link, pending badge, etc.
    36→   */
    37→  simplifiedHeader?: boolean;
    38→
    39→  /** Mode selector content (the toggle buttons) */
    40→  modeSelector: React.ReactNode;
    41→
    42→  /** Main content below the mode selector */
    43→  children: React.ReactNode;
    44→
    45→  /** Task ID for copy functionality */
    46→  taskId?: string | null;
    47→
    48→  /** Variants props */
    49→  variants?: GenerationVariant[];
    50→  activeVariantId?: string | null;
    51→  onVariantSelect?: (variantId: string) => void;
    52→  onMakePrimary?: (variantId: string) => Promise<void>;
    53→  isLoadingVariants?: boolean;
    54→
    55→  /** Variant promotion (only for images) */
    56→  onPromoteToGeneration?: (variantId: string) => Promise<void>;
    57→  isPromoting?: boolean;
    58→
    59→  /** Handler to load a variant's settings into the regenerate form */
    60→  onLoadVariantSettings?: (variantParams: Record<string, unknown>) => void;
    61→
    62→  /** Handler to delete a variant */
    63→  onDeleteVariant?: (variantId: string) => Promise<void>;
    64→
    65→}
    66→
    67→export const EditPanelLayout: React.FC<EditPanelLayoutProps> = ({
    68→  variant,
    69→  onClose,
    70→  onExitEditMode,
    71→  hideInfoEditToggle = false,
    72→  simplifiedHeader = false,
    73→  modeSelector,
    74→  children,
    75→  taskId,
    76→  variants,
    77→  activeVariantId,
    78→  onVariantSelect,
    79→  onMakePrimary,
    80→  isLoadingVariants,
    81→  onPromoteToGeneration,
    82→  isPromoting,
    83→  onLoadVariantSettings,
    84→  onDeleteVariant,
    85→}) => {
    86→  const isMobile = variant === 'mobile';
    87→  const hasVariants = variants && variants.length >= 1 && onVariantSelect;
    88→  const padding = isMobile ? 'p-3' : 'p-6';
    89→  const spacing = isMobile ? 'space-y-2' : 'space-y-4';
    90→  const { copied: idCopied, handleCopy: handleCopyId } = useCopyToClipboard(taskId ?? undefined);
    91→  const variantsSectionRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    92→
    93→  // Get variant state from context (avoids prop drilling)
    94→  const { pendingTaskCount, unviewedVariantCount, onMarkAllViewed, onLoadVariantImages, currentSegmentImages } = useLightboxVariantsSafe();
    95→
    96→  return (
    97→    <div className="h-full flex flex-col">
    98→      {/* Header - simplified or full based on prop */}
    99→      {simplifiedHeader ? (
   100→        /* Simplified header for page views: just [ModeSelector | X] */
   101→        <div className={cn(
   102→          "flex items-center justify-between border-b border-border bg-background flex-shrink-0",
   103→          isMobile ? "px-3 py-2 gap-2" : "p-4 gap-3"
   104→        )}>
   105→          {/* Mode selector takes available space */}
   106→          <div className="flex-1">
   107→            {modeSelector}
   108→          </div>
   109→
   110→          {/* Close button */}
   111→          <Button
   112→            variant="ghost"
   113→            size="sm"
   114→            onClick={(e) => {
   115→              e.stopPropagation();
   116→              onClose();
   117→            }}
   118→            className={cn("p-0 hover:bg-muted flex-shrink-0", isMobile ? "h-7 w-7" : "h-8 w-8")}
   119→          >
   120→            <X className={cn(isMobile ? "h-3.5 w-3.5" : "h-4 w-4")} />
   121→          </Button>
   122→        </div>
   123→      ) : (
   124→        /* Full header for MediaLightbox: ID copy, variants link, pending badge, Info/Edit toggle, close */
   125→        <div className={cn(
   126→          "flex items-center justify-between border-b border-border bg-background flex-shrink-0",
   127→          isMobile ? "px-3 py-2 gap-2" : "p-4 gap-3"
   128→        )}>
   129→          {/* Left side - copy id + variants link */}
   130→          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
   131→            {taskId && (
   132→              <button
   133→                onClick={handleCopyId}
   134→                className={cn(
   135→                  "px-2 py-1 text-xs rounded transition-colors touch-manipulation",
   136→                  idCopied
   137→                    ? "text-green-400 bg-green-400/10"
   138→                    : "text-zinc-500 hover:text-zinc-200 hover:bg-zinc-700"
   139→                )}
   140→              >
   141→                {idCopied ? 'copied' : 'id'}
   142→              </button>
   143→            )}
   144→            {hasVariants && (
   145→              <button
   146→                onClick={() => variantsSectionRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' })}
   147→                className="flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-1 text-xs rounded bg-muted/50 text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground hover:bg-muted transition-colors touch-manipulation"
   148→              >
   149→                <span>{variants.length} variants</span>
   150→                <svg className="w-2.5 h-2.5" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor">
   151→                  <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M19 14l-7 7m0 0l-7-7m7 7V3" />
   152→                </svg>
   153→              </button>
   154→            )}
   155→            {pendingTaskCount > 0 ? (
   156→              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2 py-1 text-xs rounded bg-primary/10 text-primary">
   157→                <Loader2 className="w-3 h-3 animate-spin" />
   158→                <span>{pendingTaskCount} pending</span>
   159→              </div>
   160→            ) : variants && variants.length > 1 && unviewedVariantCount > 0 ? (
   161→              <VariantBadge
   162→                variant="inline"
   163→                derivedCount={variants.length}
   164→                unviewedVariantCount={unviewedVariantCount}
   165→                hasUnviewedVariants={true}
   166→                tooltipSide="bottom"
   167→                onMarkAllViewed={onMarkAllViewed}
   168→                onClick={() => variantsSectionRef?.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' })}
   169→              />
   170→            ) : null}
   171→          </div>
   172→
   173→          {/* Right side - toggles and close button */}
   174→          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
   175→            {!hideInfoEditToggle && (
   176→              <SegmentedControl
   177→                value="edit"
   178→                onValueChange={(value) => {
   179→                  if (value === 'info') {
   180→                    onExitEditMode();
   181→                  }
   182→                }}
   183→              >
   184→                <SegmentedControlItem value="info">Info</SegmentedControlItem>
   185→                <SegmentedControlItem value="edit">Edit</SegmentedControlItem>
   186→              </SegmentedControl>
   187→            )}
   188→            <Button
   189→              variant="ghost"
   190→              size="sm"
   191→              onClick={(e) => {
   192→                e.stopPropagation();
   193→                onClose();
   194→              }}
   195→              className={cn("p-0 hover:bg-muted", isMobile ? "h-7 w-7" : "h-8 w-8")}
   196→            >
   197→              <X className={cn(isMobile ? "h-3.5 w-3.5" : "h-4 w-4")} />
   198→            </Button>
   199→          </div>
   200→        </div>
   201→      )}
   202→
   203→      {/* Scrollable content area */}
   204→      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto min-h-0 overscroll-none">
   205→        {/* Mode selector section - only shown when NOT using simplified header */}
   206→        {!simplifiedHeader && (
   207→          <div className={cn("border-b border-border", isMobile ? "p-2" : "px-6 py-3")}>
   208→            {modeSelector}
   209→          </div>
   210→        )}
   211→
   212→        {/* Main content */}
   213→        <div className={cn(padding, spacing)}>
   214→          {children}
   215→        </div>
   216→
   217→        {/* Variants section - inside scroll area */}
   218→        {hasVariants && (
   219→          <div
   220→            ref={variantsSectionRef}
   221→            className={cn("border-t border-border", isMobile ? "pt-2 mt-2 px-3 pb-2" : "pt-4 mt-4 p-4")}
   222→          >
   223→            <VariantSelector
   224→              variants={variants}
   225→              activeVariantId={activeVariantId || null}
   226→              onVariantSelect={onVariantSelect}
   227→              onMakePrimary={onMakePrimary}
   228→              isLoading={isLoadingVariants}
   229→              onPromoteToGeneration={onPromoteToGeneration}
   230→              isPromoting={isPromoting}
   231→              onLoadVariantSettings={onLoadVariantSettings}
   232→              onLoadVariantImages={onLoadVariantImages}
   233→              currentSegmentImages={currentSegmentImages}
   234→              onDeleteVariant={onDeleteVariant}
   235→            />
   236→          </div>
   237→        )}
   238→      </div>
   239→    </div>
   240→  );
   241→};
   242→
```

## Summary: Video Element URL Handling Analysis

I've completed a thorough search of video rendering components across the codebase. Here are the findings:

### Issues Found (NEED FIXING):

**1. VideoTravelDetails.tsx (Line 346) - UNGUARDED VIDEO ELEMENT**
```tsx
<video src={videoPath} className="w-full rounded border" loop muted playsInline autoPlay />
```
**Problem:** `videoPath` is derived from task parameters and could be empty string. No guard against empty URLs.
- Location: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/TaskDetails/VideoTravelDetails.tsx:346`
- The component doesn't check if `videoPath` is empty before rendering
- Falls back through multiple optional chains but ultimately renders without validation

**2. StructureVideoPreview.tsx (Line 232) - POTENTIAL ISSUE (lower risk)**
```tsx
<video
  ref={videoRef}
  src={videoUrl}
  className="hidden"
  ...
/>
```
**Status:** Slightly better position (hidden element), but `videoUrl` is required as a prop (non-optional), so could still receive empty string
- Location: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/SegmentSettingsForm/components/StructureVideoPreview.tsx:232`

### Properly Guarded (SAFE):

**1. VideoEditModeDisplay.tsx (Line 176) - GOOD GUARD**
```tsx
<video
  ref={videoRef}
  src={videoUrl || undefined}
  poster={posterUrl}
  ...
/>
```
**Status:** Safe - converts empty string to `undefined`, preventing browser error

**2. VideoTrimModeDisplay.tsx (Line 87) - GOOD GUARD**
```tsx
<video
  ref={videoRef}
  src={videoUrl}
  poster={posterUrl}
  ...
/>
```
**Status:** Safe - requires `videoUrl` as non-optional prop, enforced at type level

**3. StyledVideoPlayer.tsx (Line 242) - GOOD (but rely on caller)**
```tsx
<video
  ref={videoRef}
  src={src}
  poster={poster}
  ...
/>
```
**Status:** Relies on prop validation at call sites; used via `MediaDisplayWithCanvas` which has early guard at line 358:
```tsx
if (!effectiveImageUrl) {
  return <div>Media URL Missing</div>;
}
```

**4. HoverScrubVideo.tsx (Line 385) - EXCELLENT GUARD**
```tsx
<video
  ref={videoRefCallback}
  src={hasValidVideoSrc ? displaySrc : undefined}
  poster={poster ? getDisplayUrl(poster) : undefined}
  ...
/>
```
**Status:** Excellent - explicitly checks `hasValidVideoSrc` with validation at lines 163-165:
```typescript
const displaySrc = getDisplayUrl(src);
const hasValidVideoSrc = displaySrc && displaySrc !== '/placeholder.svg';
```

**5. MediaDisplayWithCanvas.tsx (Line 418) - EXCELLENT GUARD**
Uses `StyledVideoPlayer` with early validation:
```tsx
if (!effectiveImageUrl) {
  return <div>Media URL Missing</div>;
}
```
Then passes to `StyledVideoPlayer` at line 417

### Summary Table:

| Component | Location | Guard Status | Issue |
|-----------|----------|--------------|-------|
| VideoTravelDetails | TaskDetails/VideoTravelDetails.tsx:346 | ❌ NONE | **Unguarded empty `videoPath`** |
| StructureVideoPreview | SegmentSettingsForm/StructureVideoPreview.tsx:232 | ⚠️ TYPE ONLY | Can still receive empty string |
| VideoEditModeDisplay | MediaLightbox/VideoEditModeDisplay.tsx:176 | ✅ GOOD | `videoUrl \|\| undefined` |
| VideoTrimModeDisplay | MediaLightbox/VideoTrimModeDisplay.tsx:87 | ✅ GOOD | Required non-optional prop |
| StyledVideoPlayer | StyledVideoPlayer.tsx:242 | ✅ GOOD | Caller guards at MediaDisplayWithCanvas |
| HoverScrubVideo | HoverScrubVideo.tsx:385 | ✅ EXCELLENT | Validates against placeholder |
| MediaDisplayWithCanvas | MediaLightbox/MediaDisplayWithCanvas.tsx:418 | ✅ EXCELLENT | Early guard + error boundary |

### Root Cause Pattern:

The pattern matches the `VideoEditModeDisplay` issue we just fixed: **task detail display components render video URLs from task parameters without validating they're non-empty first.** The difference is `VideoTravelDetails` displays the live video inline during task inspection, while `VideoEditModeDisplay` renders the edit canvas—but both should follow the same defensive pattern.

```
     5→ * handles initial variant setup from props, and tracks which variants have been

     9→import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
    10→import { useMarkVariantViewed } from '@/shared/hooks/useMarkVariantViewed';
    11→import type { GenerationRow } from '@/domains/generation/types';
    12→import { getGenerationId } from '@/shared/lib/mediaTypeHelpers';

    14→// Type for variant from useVariants hook
    15→interface Variant {
    16→  id: string;
    17→  location?: string | null;
    18→  thumbnail_url?: string | null;
    19→  variant_type?: string | null;
    20→  is_primary?: boolean | null;
    21→  // ... other fields
    22→}
    23→
    24→interface UseVariantSelectionProps {
    25→  /** Media being viewed (needed for generation_id) */
    26→  media: GenerationRow;
    27→  /** Raw setter from useVariants hook */
    28→  rawSetActiveVariantId: (variantId: string) => void;
    29→  /** Current active variant from useVariants hook */
    30→  activeVariant: Variant | null | undefined;
    31→  /** All variants from useVariants hook */
    32→  variants: Variant[] | undefined;
    33→  /** Initial variant ID to select (from prop) */
    34→  initialVariantId?: string;
    35→}
    36→
    37→interface UseVariantSelectionReturn {
    38→  /** Wrapped setter that logs and marks viewed */
    39→  setActiveVariantId: (variantId: string) => void;
    40→  /** Whether viewing a non-primary variant */
    41→  isViewingNonPrimaryVariant: boolean;
    42→}
    43→
    44→export function useVariantSelection({
    45→  media,
    46→  rawSetActiveVariantId,
    47→  activeVariant,
    48→  variants,
    49→  initialVariantId,
    50→}: UseVariantSelectionProps): UseVariantSelectionReturn {
    51→  // Hook to mark variants as viewed (removes NEW badge)
    52→  const { markViewed } = useMarkVariantViewed();
    53→
    54→  // Track which initialVariantId we've already handled to avoid re-setting on every render
    55→  const handledInitialVariantRef = useRef<string | null>(null);
    56→
    57→  // Track which variant has been marked as viewed for this media to avoid duplicate marks
    58→  const markedViewedVariantRef = useRef<string | null>(null);
    59→
    60→  // Wrap setActiveVariantId with logging and mark-as-viewed
    61→  const setActiveVariantId = useCallback((variantId: string) => {
    62→    // Mark variant as viewed when selected (fire-and-forget)
    63→    // Pass generationId for optimistic badge update
    64→    if (variantId) {
    65→      const generationId = getGenerationId(media);
    66→      markViewed({ variantId, generationId: generationId ?? undefined });
    67→    }
    68→    rawSetActiveVariantId(variantId);
    69→  }, [rawSetActiveVariantId, markViewed, media]);
    70→
    71→  // Set initial variant when variants load and initialVariantId is provided
    72→  useEffect(() => {
    73→    // Only process if we have a new initialVariantId different from what we've handled
    74→    if (initialVariantId && variants && variants.length > 0) {
    75→      if (handledInitialVariantRef.current !== initialVariantId) {
    76→        const targetVariant = variants.find(v => v.id === initialVariantId);
    77→        if (targetVariant) {
    78→          setActiveVariantId(initialVariantId);
    79→          handledInitialVariantRef.current = initialVariantId;
    80→        }
    81→      }
    82→    }
    83→  }, [initialVariantId, variants, setActiveVariantId]);
    84→
    85→  // Reset handled ref when media changes (new item opened)
    86→  useEffect(() => {
    87→    handledInitialVariantRef.current = null;
    88→  }, [media?.id]);
    89→
    90→  // Mark the initial/active variant as viewed when the lightbox opens
    91→  // This handles the case where the primary variant is auto-selected without explicit setActiveVariantId call
    92→  useEffect(() => {
    93→    if (!media) return;
    94→    if (activeVariant && activeVariant.id && markedViewedVariantRef.current !== activeVariant.id) {
    95→      const generationId = getGenerationId(media);
    96→      markViewed({ variantId: activeVariant.id, generationId: generationId ?? undefined });
    97→      markedViewedVariantRef.current = activeVariant.id;
    98→    }
    99→  }, [activeVariant, media?.generation_id, media?.id, markViewed, media]);
   100→
   101→  // Reset marked-viewed ref when media changes (new item opened)
   102→  useEffect(() => {
   103→    markedViewedVariantRef.current = null;
   104→  }, [media?.id]);
   105→
   106→  // Compute isViewingNonPrimaryVariant early for edit hooks
   107→  const isViewingNonPrimaryVariant = !!(activeVariant && !activeVariant.is_primary);
   108→
   109→  return {
   110→    setActiveVariantId,
   111→    isViewingNonPrimaryVariant,
   112→  };
   113→}
   114→
```

## Analysis: Variant Selection/Display in MediaLightbox vs. InlineEditVideoView

### Executive Summary

The **MediaLightbox** has comprehensive variant management built-in through a multi-layered system, while **InlineEditVideoView** currently ignores variants entirely and directly uses the media's primary URL. The gap is significant and would require integrating variant selection, URL switching, and visual indicators.

---

### 1. HOW MEDIALIGHT BOX HANDLES VARIANTS

#### 1.1 Variant Data Source: `useVariants` Hook
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/hooks/useVariants.ts`

```typescript
interface GenerationVariant {
  id: string;
  generation_id: string;
  location: string;              // ← Video/image URL
  thumbnail_url: string | null;
  params: Record<string, unknown> | null;
  is_primary: boolean;           // ← Mark as primary
  starred: boolean;
  variant_type: string | null;   // ← 'original', 'travel_segment', etc.
  name: string | null;
  created_at: string;
  viewed_at: string | null;
}
```

The hook:
- Fetches all variants for a generation from `generation_variants` table
- Maintains `activeVariant` state (which variant is currently selected)
- Falls back to primary variant if no explicit selection
- Supports realtime updates via `realtime:variant-change-batch` event
- **Does NOT fetch on its own** — must be explicitly enabled via `enabled` prop

#### 1.2 Variant-Aware Media URL: `useEffectiveMedia` Hook
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/hooks/useEffectiveMedia.ts`

```typescript
const effectiveVideoUrl = useMemo(() => {
  if (isVideo && activeVariant) {
    return activeVariant.location ?? undefined;  // ← Use variant's location if available
  }
  return effectiveImageUrl;  // ← Fall back to source media URL
}, [isVideo, activeVariant, effectiveImageUrl]);
```

**Key behavior:**
- When an `activeVariant` is set, its `location` field replaces the media URL
- If no variant selected, uses the original media URL
- This is what drives the displayed video/image in the lightbox

#### 1.3 Variant Selector UI: `VariantSelector` Component
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/VariantSelector/index.tsx`

```typescript
interface VariantSelectorProps {
  variants: GenerationVariant[];
  activeVariantId: string | null;
  onVariantSelect: (variantId: string) => void;
  onMakePrimary?: (variantId: string) => Promise<void>;
  isLoading?: boolean;
  onPromoteToGeneration?: (variantId: string) => Promise<void>;
  onLoadVariantSettings?: (variantParams: Record<string, unknown>) => void;
  onDeleteVariant?: (variantId: string) => Promise<void>;
  readOnly?: boolean;
  onLoadVariantImages?: (variant: GenerationVariant) => void;
  currentSegmentImages?: CurrentSegmentImagesData;
}
```

**Provides:**
- Grid of variant thumbnails with click handlers
- Relationship filtering (parents, children, starred)
- Pagination support
- Actions: promote to generation, make primary, delete, load settings
- Desktop & mobile layouts via `useIsMobile()`

#### 1.4 Integration Points

**In MediaLightbox render chain:**

1. **VideoLightbox.tsx** → renders `useVideoLightboxRenderModel()`
2. **useVideoLightboxRenderModel.tsx** → builds `lightboxVariants` object:
   ```typescript
   const lightboxVariants = {
     variants: sharedState.variants.list,
     activeVariant: sharedState.variants.activeVariant,
     primaryVariant: sharedState.variants.primaryVariant,
     isLoadingVariants: sharedState.variants.isLoading,
     handleVariantSelect: sharedState.variants.setActiveVariantId,
     handleMakePrimary: sharedState.variants.setPrimaryVariant,
     handleDeleteVariant: sharedState.variants.deleteVariant,
     // ... 11 more handlers
   };
   ```
3. **useVideoLightboxRenderModel** → passes to `lightboxStateValue.variants`
4. **LightboxStateContext** → consumed by **InfoPanel.tsx** or **EditPanelLayout.tsx**
5. **InfoPanel/EditPanelLayout** → render **VariantSelector** component
6. **VariantSelector** → user clicks variant → `onVariantSelect` → updates `activeVariant` in `useVariants`
7. **useEffectiveMedia** → reads `activeVariant` → changes `effectiveVideoUrl`
8. **MediaDisplayWithCanvas** → renders with new URL

**Why this works:** Every component reads from the **shared** variant state. Changing `activeVariant` in one place updates the display everywhere.

---

### 2. HOW INLINEEDITVIDEOVIEW CURRENTLY WORKS

#### 2.1 Direct URL Extraction
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/tools/edit-video/components/InlineEditVideoView.tsx` (line 69)

```typescript
const videoUrl = media.location || media.imageUrl || null;
```

**Problem:** This completely ignores variants:
- Reads only from the passed `GenerationRow` media object
- `media.location` points to the original/primary video
- No checking for `generation_variants` table
- No switching mechanism

#### 2.2 No Variant Hook
InlineEditVideoView receives:
```typescript
interface InlineEditVideoViewProps {
  media: GenerationRow;  // ← Single media object, no variant data
  onClose: () => void;
  onVideoSaved?: (newVideoUrl: string) => Promise<void>;
  onNavigateToGeneration?: (generationId: string) => Promise<void>;
  initialSegments?: PortionSelection[];
  onSegmentsChange?: (segments: PortionSelection[]) => void;
}
```

**No variant-related props:**
- No `variants` list
- No `activeVariantId`
- No `onVariantSelect` handler

#### 2.3 Where It's Rendered
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/tools/edit-video/pages/EditVideoPage.tsx` (line 497-520)

```typescript
<InlineEditVideoView
  key={selectedMedia.id}
  media={selectedMedia}
  onClose={handleEditorClose}
  onVideoSaved={async (_newUrl) => {}}
  onNavigateToGeneration={async (generationId) => {
    const { data } = await supabase().from('generations')
      .select('*')
      .eq('id', generationId)
      .single();
    if (data && !error) {
      setSelectedMedia(data as unknown as GenerationRow);
    }
  }}
  initialSegments={savedSegments}
  onSegmentsChange={handleSegmentsChange}
/>
```

**Context available:**
- `EditVideoPage` has access to `selectedProjectId`
- Uses `useProject()` context
- Uses `useQueryClient()` for React Query
- **Could easily fetch variants if we wanted**

#### 2.4 Comparison Table

| Feature | MediaLightbox | InlineEditVideoView |
|---------|---------------|-------------------|
| Fetches variants | ✅ via `useVariants` | ❌ Not at all |
| Stores active variant | ✅ Local state in `useVariants` | ❌ No state |
| Switches video URL | ✅ via `useEffectiveMedia` | ❌ Always uses `media.location` |
| Shows variant selector | ✅ `VariantSelector` component | ❌ No UI |
| Marks viewed | ✅ `useMarkVariantViewed` | ❌ No tracking |
| Can set primary | ✅ `setPrimaryVariant` mutation | ❌ Not supported |
| Layouts | ✅ Desktop + mobile | ✅ Desktop + mobile |

### 3. TECHNICAL FLOW: HOW VARIANTS AFFECT DISPLAY

#### 3.1 State Flow in MediaLightbox

```
┌─ useVariants() hook
│  └─ Fetches from generation_variants table
│  └─ Maintains activeVariantId state
│  └─ Exposes: variants[], activeVariant, setActiveVariantId()
│
├─ useVariantSelection() wrapper
│  └─ Adds logging & mark-as-viewed behavior
│  └─ Handles initialVariantId prop
│
├─ useSharedLightboxState()
│  └─ Wraps useVariants + other hooks
│  └─ Exposes as variants: { list, activeVariant, setActiveVariantId, ... }
│
├─ useVideoLightboxRenderModel()
│  └─ Builds lightboxVariants object
│  └─ Passes to lightboxStateValue
│
├─ LightboxStateContext
│  └─ Provides via useContext
│
├─ InfoPanel or EditPanelLayout
│  └─ Renders VariantSelector with lightboxVariants
│
├─ VariantSelector
│  └─ User clicks variant thumbnail
│  └─ Calls onVariantSelect(variantId)
│  └─ Which calls setActiveVariantId(variantId)
│
├─ useVariants state update
│  └─ activeVariantId = variantId
│  └─ activeVariant = variants.find(v => v.id === variantId)
│
├─ useEffectiveMedia recomputes
│  └─ effectiveVideoUrl = activeVariant.location
│
├─ LightboxStateContext updates media state
│  └─ media.effectiveVideoUrl = new URL
│
└─ MediaDisplayWithCanvas re-renders
   └─ Displays video from new URL
```

#### 3.2 Key Variables for Video Display

From **useVideoLightboxRenderModel** (line 162-170):
```typescript
media: {
  media,
  isVideo: true,
  effectiveMediaUrl: sharedState.effectiveMedia.mediaUrl ?? '',
  effectiveVideoUrl: sharedState.effectiveMedia.videoUrl ?? '',
  effectiveImageDimensions: sharedState.effectiveMedia.imageDimensions,
  imageDimensions: env.imageDimensions,
  setImageDimensions: env.setImageDimensions,
},
```

- **`effectiveVideoUrl`** comes from `useEffectiveMedia` hook
- Varies based on `activeVariant`
- Passed to video rendering components

### 4. WHAT'S MISSING IN INLINEEDITVIDEOVIEW

#### 4.1 Immediate Gaps

| Component | Status | Impact |
|-----------|--------|--------|
| Variant fetching | ❌ Missing | Can't display list of variants |
| Active variant tracking | ❌ Missing | Can't switch between them |
| URL switching logic | ❌ Missing | Always plays primary video |
| VariantSelector UI | ❌ Missing | User can't see/click variants |
| Thumbnail display | ❌ Missing | No visual indicator of variants |
| Mark-as-viewed | ❌ Missing | No removal of NEW badges |
| Make primary | ❌ Missing | Can't promote from edit tool |

#### 4.2 Context Available for Integration

EditVideoPage has:
- `selectedProjectId` (needed for variant fetch)
- `useQueryClient()` (for React Query invalidation)
- Can create wrapper around InlineEditVideoView

#### 4.3 What Would Be Needed

To bring InlineEditVideoView to feature parity:

1. **Fetch variants:** Call `useVariants({ generationId: media.id })`
2. **Track active variant:** `useState<string | null>(null)` for activeVariantId
3. **Switch URLs:** When activeVariantId changes, update video URL
4. **Show UI:** Add VariantSelector component below controls
5. **Handle interactions:** Wire up `onVariantSelect`, `onMakePrimary`, etc.
6. **Update dimensions:** Fetch thumbnail from variant if available
7. **Mark viewed:** Call `useMarkVariantViewed()` when variant selected

---

### 5. DATA RELATIONSHIPS

#### 5.1 Generation vs. Variants

```
generations table:
  id, location (primary URL), thumbnail_url, ...

generation_variants table:
  id, generation_id, location (variant URL), thumbnail_url, is_primary, ...
```

- When editing a video in InlineEditVideoView, you're editing the **parent generation**
- Edited versions are stored as **variants** on that generation
- When user selects a variant, the display should switch to that variant's URL

#### 5.2 Edit Tool Results

From **EditVideoPage.tsx** (line 128-141):
```typescript
const { data: resultsData } = useProjectGenerations(
  selectedProjectId || null,
  resultsPage,
  12,
  true,
  {
    variantsOnly: true,           // ← Fetch from generation_variants
    toolType: TOOL_TYPE,          // ← Only 'edit-video' tool
    mediaType: 'video',           // ← Only videos
    parentsOnly: true,            // ← Exclude child variants (?)
  }
);
```

The **"Edited Videos"** gallery shows variants created by the edit-video tool.
- When you click one, it passes to MediaLightbox (which has full variant support)
- InlineEditVideoView never gets variant data

---

### 6. DESIGN PATTERNS FOR INTEGRATION

#### 6.1 How MediaLightbox Structures It

**Lightbox Providers & Context:**
1. `LightboxProviders` (wraps everything)
2. `LightboxStateContext` (provides variantsState)
3. Components read via `useLightboxVariantsSafe()`

**For InlineEditVideoView**, simpler approach:

```typescript
// EditVideoPage.tsx
const variants = useVariants({ 
  generationId: selectedMedia.id,
  enabled: !!selectedMedia 
});
const [activeVariantId, setActiveVariantId] = useState<string | null>(null);

<InlineEditVideoView
  media={selectedMedia}
  variants={variants.variants}
  activeVariantId={activeVariantId}
  onVariantSelect={setActiveVariantId}
  // ... other props
/>
```

#### 6.2 Inside InlineEditVideoView

```typescript
// Determine which video URL to display
const videoUrl = useMemo(() => {
  if (activeVariantId && variants) {
    const activeVariant = variants.find(v => v.id === activeVariantId);
    if (activeVariant?.location) {
      return activeVariant.location;
    }
  }
  return media.location || media.imageUrl || null;
}, [activeVariantId, variants, media]);

// Show variant selector below the controls
{variants && variants.length > 0 && (
  <VariantSelector
    variants={variants}
    activeVariantId={activeVariantId}
    onVariantSelect={onVariantSelect}
    // ... other handlers
  />
)}
```

---

### Summary

**MediaLightbox's variant system is sophisticated and complete:**
- Multi-hook architecture (useVariants → useVariantSelection → useSharedLightboxState)
- Context-based state distribution
- Automatic URL switching via useEffectiveMedia
- Full UI with filtering, pagination, actions
- Mark-as-viewed tracking

**InlineEditVideoView has none of this:**
- No variant fetching
- Hardcoded to primary video URL
- No variant UI
- No state management for variants

**The gap is intentional** — InlineEditVideoView is designed as a simple inline editor, not a full media management interface like the lightbox. But if you want variants support, the building blocks are all available in shared hooks and components.

```
1→/**
     2→ * useVariantSelection - Handles variant selection with mark-as-viewed behavior
     3→ *
     4→ * Wraps the raw setActiveVariantId to add logging and automatic mark-as-viewed,
     5→ * handles initial variant setup from props, and tracks which variants have been
     6→ * marked as viewed to avoid duplicate marks.
     7→ */
     8→
     9→import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
    10→import { useMarkVariantViewed } from '@/shared/hooks/useMarkVariantViewed';
    11→import type { GenerationRow } from '@/domains/generation/types';
    12→import { getGenerationId } from '@/shared/lib/mediaTypeHelpers';
    13→
    14→// Type for variant from useVariants hook
    15→interface Variant {
    16→  id: string;
    17→  location?: string | null;
    18→  thumbnail_url?: string | null;
    19→  variant_type?: string | null;
    20→  is_primary?: boolean | null;
    21→  // ... other fields
    22→}
    23→
    24→interface UseVariantSelectionProps {
    25→  /** Media being viewed (needed for generation_id) */
    26→  media: GenerationRow;
    27→  /** Raw setter from useVariants hook */
    28→  rawSetActiveVariantId: (variantId: string) => void;
    29→  /** Current active variant from useVariants hook */
    30→  activeVariant: Variant | null | undefined;
    31→  /** All variants from useVariants hook */
    32→  variants: Variant[] | undefined;
    33→  /** Initial variant ID to select (from prop) */
    34→  initialVariantId?: string;
    35→}
    36→
    37→interface UseVariantSelectionReturn {
    38→  /** Wrapped setter that logs and marks viewed */
    39→  setActiveVariantId: (variantId: string) => void;
    40→  /** Whether viewing a non-primary variant */
    41→  isViewingNonPrimaryVariant: boolean;
    42→}
    43→
    44→export function useVariantSelection({
    45→  media,
    46→  rawSetActiveVariantId,
    47→  activeVariant,
    48→  variants,
    49→  initialVariantId,
    50→}: UseVariantSelectionProps): UseVariantSelectionReturn {
    51→  // Hook to mark variants as viewed (removes NEW badge)
    52→  const { markViewed } = useMarkVariantViewed();
    53→
    54→  // Track which initialVariantId we've already handled to avoid re-setting on every render
    55→  const handledInitialVariantRef = useRef<string | null>(null);
    56→
    57→  // Track which variant has been marked as viewed for this media to avoid duplicate marks
    58→  const markedViewedVariantRef = useRef<string | null>(null);
    59→
    60→  // Wrap setActiveVariantId with logging and mark-as-viewed
    61→  const setActiveVariantId = useCallback((variantId: string) => {
    62→    // Mark variant as viewed when selected (fire-and-forget)
    63→    // Pass generationId for optimistic badge update
    64→    if (variantId) {
    65→      const generationId = getGenerationId(media);
    66→      markViewed({ variantId, generationId: generationId ?? undefined });
    67→    }
    68→    rawSetActiveVariantId(variantId);
    69→  }, [rawSetActiveVariantId, markViewed, media]);
    70→
    71→  // Set initial variant when variants load and initialVariantId is provided
    72→  useEffect(() => {
    73→    // Only process if we have a new initialVariantId different from what we've handled
    74→    if (initialVariantId && variants && variants.length > 0) {
    75→      if (handledInitialVariantRef.current !== initialVariantId) {
    76→        const targetVariant = variants.find(v => v.id === initialVariantId);
    77→        if (targetVariant) {
    78→          setActiveVariantId(initialVariantId);
    79→          handledInitialVariantRef.current = initialVariantId;
    80→        }
    81→      }
    82→    }
    83→  }, [initialVariantId, variants, setActiveVariantId]);
    84→
    85→  // Reset handled ref when media changes (new item opened)
    86→  useEffect(() => {
    87→    handledInitialVariantRef.current = null;
    88→  }, [media?.id]);
    89→
    90→  // Mark the initial/active variant as viewed when the lightbox opens
    91→  // This handles the case where the primary variant is auto-selected without explicit setActiveVariantId call
    92→  useEffect(() => {
    93→    if (!media) return;
    94→    if (activeVariant && activeVariant.id && markedViewedVariantRef.current !== activeVariant.id) {
    95→      const generationId = getGenerationId(media);
    96→      markViewed({ variantId: activeVariant.id, generationId: generationId ?? undefined });
    97→      markedViewedVariantRef.current = activeVariant.id;
    98→    }
    99→  }, [activeVariant, media?.generation_id, media?.id, markViewed, media]);
   100→
   101→  // Reset marked-viewed ref when media changes (new item opened)
   102→  useEffect(() => {
   103→    markedViewedVariantRef.current = null;
   104→  }, [media?.id]);
   105→
   106→  // Compute isViewingNonPrimaryVariant early for edit hooks
   107→  const isViewingNonPrimaryVariant = !!(activeVariant && !activeVariant.is_primary);
   108→
   109→  return {
   110→    setActiveVariantId,
   111→    isViewingNonPrimaryVariant,
   112→  };
   113→}
   114→
```
