```
1→/**
     2→ * useVariants Hook
     3→ *
     4→ * Centralized hook for fetching and managing variants for a generation.
     5→ * Allows switching between variants and setting the primary variant.
     6→ * Supports realtime updates via RealtimeProvider.
     7→ *
     8→ * Used by: MediaLightbox, InlineEditView, and other components that display variants.
     9→ */
    10→
    11→import { useState, useCallback, useMemo, useEffect } from 'react';
    12→import { useQuery, useMutation, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
    13→import { getSupabaseClient as supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';
    14→import { invalidateVariantChange } from '@/shared/hooks/invalidation/useGenerationInvalidation';
    15→import { normalizeAndPresentError } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandling/runtimeError';
    16→import { generationQueryKeys } from '@/shared/lib/queryKeys/generations';
    17→import { useAppEventListener } from '@/shared/lib/typedEvents';
    18→
    19→/**
    20→ * A variant of a generation (from generation_variants table)
    21→ */
    22→export interface GenerationVariant {
    23→  id: string;
    24→  generation_id: string;
    25→  location: string;
    26→  thumbnail_url: string | null;
    27→  params: Record<string, unknown> | null;
    28→  is_primary: boolean;
    29→  starred: boolean;
    30→  variant_type: string | null;
    31→  name: string | null;
    32→  created_at: string;
    33→  viewed_at: string | null;
    34→}
    35→
    36→/**
    37→ * Return type for useVariants hook
    38→ */
    39→interface UseVariantsReturn {
    40→  variants: GenerationVariant[];
    41→  primaryVariant: GenerationVariant | null;
    42→  activeVariant: GenerationVariant | null;
    43→  isLoading: boolean;
    44→  error: Error | null;
    45→  refetch: () => void;
    46→  setActiveVariantId: (variantId: string | null) => void;
    47→  setPrimaryVariant: (variantId: string) => Promise<void>;
    48→  deleteVariant: (variantId: string) => Promise<void>;
    49→}
    50→
    51→interface UseVariantsProps {
    52→  generationId: string | null;
    53→  enabled?: boolean;
    54→}
    55→
    56→export const useVariants = ({
    57→  generationId,
    58→  enabled = true,
    59→}: UseVariantsProps): UseVariantsReturn => {
    60→  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
    61→  const [activeVariantId, setActiveVariantIdInternal] = useState<string | null>(null);
    62→  
    63→  // Stable callback - no deps needed since we just forward to internal setter
    64→  const setActiveVariantId = useCallback((variantId: string | null) => {
    65→    setActiveVariantIdInternal(variantId);
    66→  }, []);
    67→
    68→  // Fetch variants for this generation
    69→  const {
    70→    data: variants = [],
    71→    isLoading,
    72→    error,
    73→    refetch,
    74→  } = useQuery({
    75→    queryKey: generationId ? generationQueryKeys.variants(generationId) : ['generation-variants', null],
    76→    queryFn: async () => {
    77→      if (!generationId) return [];
    78→
    79→      const { data, error } = await supabase().from('generation_variants')
    80→        .select('*')
    81→        .eq('generation_id', generationId)
    82→        .order('created_at', { ascending: false });
    83→
    84→      if (error) {
    85→        console.error('[useVariants] Error fetching variants:', error);
    86→        throw error;
    87→      }
    88→
    89→      return (data || []) as GenerationVariant[];
    90→    },
    91→    enabled: enabled && !!generationId,
    92→    staleTime: 30000, // 30 seconds
    93→  });
    94→
    95→  // Listen for realtime variant changes and refetch when our generationId is affected
    96→  const handleVariantChange = useCallback((detail: { affectedGenerationIds: string[] }) => {
    97→    if (!generationId || !enabled) return;
    98→    const affectedIds = detail?.affectedGenerationIds || [];
    99→    if (affectedIds.includes(generationId)) {
   100→      refetch();
   101→    }
   102→  }, [generationId, enabled, refetch]);
   103→
   104→  useAppEventListener('realtime:variant-change-batch', handleVariantChange);
   105→
   106→  // Find the primary variant
   107→  const primaryVariant = useMemo(() => {
   108→    return variants.find((v) => v.is_primary) || null;
   109→  }, [variants]);
   110→
   111→  // Get the active variant (selected or primary, fallback to first variant)
   112→  const activeVariant = useMemo(() => {
   113→    if (activeVariantId) {
   114→      const found = variants.find((v) => v.id === activeVariantId);
   115→      if (found) {
   116→        return found;
   117→      }
   118→    }
   119→    // Fall back to primary, then first variant if no primary exists
   120→    return primaryVariant || variants[0] || null;
   121→  }, [variants, activeVariantId, primaryVariant]);
   122→
   123→  // Initialize active variant to primary (or first variant) when variants load
   124→  // NOTE: Using useEffect, not useMemo - side effects should not be in useMemo
   125→  useEffect(() => {
   126→    if (!activeVariantId && variants.length > 0) {
   127→      const variantToSelect = primaryVariant || variants[0];
   128→      if (variantToSelect) {
   129→        setActiveVariantIdInternal(variantToSelect.id);
   130→      }
   131→    }
   132→  }, [primaryVariant, variants, activeVariantId]);
   133→
   134→  // Mutation to set a variant as primary
   135→  const setPrimaryMutation = useMutation({
   136→    mutationFn: async (variantId: string) => {
   137→
   138→      // Update the variant to be primary
   139→      // The database trigger will handle unsetting the old primary
   140→      const { error } = await supabase().from('generation_variants')
   141→        .update({ is_primary: true })
   142→        .eq('id', variantId);
   143→
   144→      if (error) {
   145→        console.error('[useVariants] Error setting primary variant:', error);
   146→        throw error;
   147→      }
   148→
   149→      return variantId;
   150→    },
   151→    onSuccess: async (variantId) => {
   152→
   153→      // Invalidate caches using centralized function
   154→      if (generationId) {
   155→        await invalidateVariantChange(queryClient, {
   156→          generationId,
   157→          reason: 'set-primary-variant',
   158→        });
   159→      }
   160→
   161→      // SINGLE-SEGMENT PROPAGATION: If this is a child of a single-segment parent,
   162→      // also create a variant on the parent so the main generation updates
   163→      try {
   164→        // Get the promoted variant's location and thumbnail
   165→        const { data: promotedVariant } = await supabase().from('generation_variants')
   166→          .select('location, thumbnail_url, params')
   167→          .eq('id', variantId)
   168→          .single();
   169→
   170→        if (!promotedVariant || !generationId) return;
   171→
   172→        // Check if this generation is a child with a parent
   173→        const { data: generation } = await supabase().from('generations')
   174→          .select('is_child, parent_generation_id')
   175→          .eq('id', generationId)
   176→          .single();
   177→
   178→        if (!generation?.is_child || !generation?.parent_generation_id) return;
   179→
   180→        // Count how many children the parent has
   181→        const { count: childCount } = await supabase().from('generations')
   182→          .select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true })
   183→          .eq('parent_generation_id', generation.parent_generation_id)
   184→          .eq('is_child', true);
   185→
   186→        // Only propagate for single-segment parents
   187→        if (childCount !== 1) {
   188→          return;
   189→        }
   190→
   191→        // Create a new variant on the parent with the promoted video
   192→        // Exclude task-specific fields that shouldn't propagate to parent
   193→        const {
   194→          source_task_id: _sourceTaskId,
   195→          child_generation_id: _childGenId,
   196→          ...paramsToPropagate
   197→        } = (promotedVariant.params || {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
   198→
   199→        const { error: insertError } = await supabase().from('generation_variants')
   200→          .insert({
   201→            generation_id: generation.parent_generation_id,
   202→            location: promotedVariant.location,
   203→            thumbnail_url: promotedVariant.thumbnail_url,
   204→            is_primary: true, // DB trigger will unset old primary
   205→            variant_type: 'travel_segment',
   206→            params: {
   207→              ...paramsToPropagate,
   208→              propagated_from_child: generationId,
   209→              propagated_from_variant: variantId,
   210→            },
   211→          });
   212→
   213→        if (insertError) {
   214→          normalizeAndPresentError(insertError, { context: 'useVariants', showToast: false });
   215→        } else {
   216→          // Invalidate parent's variant cache
   217→          await invalidateVariantChange(queryClient, {
   218→            generationId: generation.parent_generation_id,
   219→            reason: 'single-segment-propagation',
   220→          });
   221→        }
   222→      } catch (err) {
   223→        console.error('[useVariants] Error in single-segment propagation:', err);
   224→        // Don't throw - this is a nice-to-have, not critical
   225→      }
   226→    },
   227→    onError: (error) => {
   228→      normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'useVariants.setPrimaryVariant', toastTitle: 'Failed to set primary variant' });
   229→    },
   230→  });
   231→
   232→  const setPrimaryVariant = useCallback(
   233→    async (variantId: string) => {
   234→      await setPrimaryMutation.mutateAsync(variantId);
   235→    },
   236→    [setPrimaryMutation]
   237→  );
   238→
   239→  // Mutation to delete a variant
   240→  const deleteVariantMutation = useMutation({
   241→    mutationFn: async (variantId: string) => {
   242→
   243→      // Check if variant is primary - don't allow deleting primary
   244→      const variant = variants.find(v => v.id === variantId);
   245→      if (variant?.is_primary) {
   246→        throw new Error('Cannot delete the primary variant');
   247→      }
   248→
   249→      const { error } = await supabase().from('generation_variants')
   250→        .delete()
   251→        .eq('id', variantId);
   252→
   253→      if (error) {
   254→        console.error('[useVariants] Error deleting variant:', error);
   255→        throw error;
   256→      }
   257→
   258→      return variantId;
   259→    },
   260→    onSuccess: async (variantId) => {
   261→
   262→      // If deleted variant was active, switch to primary
   263→      if (activeVariantId === variantId) {
   264→        setActiveVariantId(primaryVariant?.id || null);
   265→      }
   266→
   267→      // Invalidate caches
   268→      if (generationId) {
   269→        await invalidateVariantChange(queryClient, {
   270→          generationId,
   271→          reason: 'delete-variant',
   272→        });
   273→      }
   274→    },
   275→    onError: (error) => {
   276→      normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'useVariants.deleteVariant', toastTitle: 'Failed to delete variant' });
   277→    },
   278→  });
   279→
   280→  const deleteVariant = useCallback(
   281→    async (variantId: string) => {
   282→      await deleteVariantMutation.mutateAsync(variantId);
   283→    },
   284→    [deleteVariantMutation]
   285→  );
   286→
   287→  return {
   288→    variants,
   289→    primaryVariant,
   290→    activeVariant,
   291→    isLoading,
   292→    error: error as Error | null,
   293→    refetch,
   294→    setActiveVariantId,
   295→    setPrimaryVariant,
   296→    deleteVariant,
   297→  };
   298→};
   299→
```

```
1→/**
     2→ * usePromoteVariantToGeneration Hook
     3→ *
     4→ * Creates a new generation record from an existing variant.
     5→ * This allows variants to be promoted to standalone generations that can
     6→ * be added to shots, starred, and treated as first-class gallery items.
     7→ *
     8→ * Used by:
     9→ * - VariantSelector "Make new image" button
    10→ * - ShotSelectorControls "Add as new image to shot" button
    11→ */
    12→
    13→import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
    14→import { getSupabaseClient as supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';
    15→import type { Json } from '@/integrations/supabase/types';

    20→interface PromoteVariantParams {
    21→  /** ID of the variant to promote */
    22→  variantId: string;
    23→  /** Project ID for the new generation */
    24→  projectId: string;
    25→}
    26→
    27→interface PromotedGeneration {
    28→  id: string;
    29→  location: string;
    30→  thumbnail_url: string | null;
    31→  type: string;
    32→  project_id: string;
    33→  based_on: string;
    34→  params: Record<string, Json | undefined>;
    35→}
    36→
    37→function toJsonObject(value: unknown): Record<string, Json | undefined> {
    38→  if (value && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value)) {
    39→    return value as Record<string, Json | undefined>;
    40→  }
    41→  return {};
    42→}
    43→
    44→/**
    45→ * Hook for promoting a variant to a standalone generation
    46→ */
    47→export const usePromoteVariantToGeneration = () => {
    48→  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
    49→
    50→  return useMutation({
    51→    mutationFn: async ({
    52→      variantId,
    53→      projectId,
    54→    }: PromoteVariantParams): Promise<PromotedGeneration> => {
    55→      // 1. Fetch the variant — its generation_id is the single source of truth for lineage
    56→      const { data: variant, error: variantError } = await supabase().from('generation_variants')
    57→        .select('*')
    58→        .eq('id', variantId)
    59→        .single();
    60→
    61→      if (variantError || !variant) {
    62→        throw new Error(`Failed to fetch variant: ${variantError?.message || 'Not found'}`);
    63→      }
    64→
    65→      const sourceGenerationId = variant.generation_id;
    66→      if (!sourceGenerationId) {
    67→        throw new Error('Variant is missing generation_id');
    68→      }
    69→
    70→      // 2. Determine if this is a video or image based on the URL
    71→      const isVideo = hasVideoExtension(variant.location);
    72→      const mediaType = isVideo ? 'video' : 'image';
    73→
    74→      // 3. Create new generation record
    75→      // Strip generation_id from variant params — if it leaks into the new generation's params,
    76→      // generationTransformers spreads params into metadata, and getGenerationId() reads
    77→      // metadata.generation_id (priority 2) over media.id (priority 3), causing the lightbox
    78→      // to fetch variants for the SOURCE generation instead of the new one.
    79→      const {
    80→        generation_id: _stripGenId,
    81→        source_task_id: _stripTaskId,
    82→        ...cleanVariantParams
    83→      } = toJsonObject(variant.params);
    84→
    85→      const toolType = typeof cleanVariantParams.tool_type === 'string'
    86→        ? cleanVariantParams.tool_type
    87→        : 'promoted-variant';
    88→
    89→      const newGenerationData = {
    90→        location: variant.location,
    91→        thumbnail_url: variant.thumbnail_url,
    92→        project_id: projectId,
    93→        type: mediaType,
    94→        based_on: sourceGenerationId,
    95→        params: {
    96→          ...cleanVariantParams,
    97→          source: 'variant_promotion',
    98→          source_variant_id: variantId,
    99→          source_generation_id: sourceGenerationId,
   100→          tool_type: toolType,
   101→          promoted_at: new Date().toISOString(),
   102→        },
   103→      };
   104→
   105→      const { data: newGeneration, error: insertError } = await supabase().from('generations')
   106→        .insert(newGenerationData)
   107→        .select()
   108→        .single();
   109→
   110→      if (insertError || !newGeneration) {
   111→        throw new Error(`Failed to create generation: ${insertError?.message || 'Unknown error'}`);
   112→      }
   113→
   114→      // DB trigger trg_auto_create_variant_after_generation auto-creates
   115→      // the primary variant from the generation's location/params.
   116→
   117→      return {
   118→        id: newGeneration.id,
   119→        location: newGeneration.location ?? variant.location,
   120→        thumbnail_url: newGeneration.thumbnail_url,
   121→        type: newGeneration.type ?? mediaType,
   122→        project_id: newGeneration.project_id ?? projectId,
   123→        based_on: newGeneration.based_on ?? sourceGenerationId,
   124→        params: toJsonObject(newGeneration.params),
   125→      };
   126→    },
   127→
   128→    onSuccess: (data) => {
   129→      // Invalidate generations queries to show the new generation in galleries
   130→      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ [REDACTED] });
   131→      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ [REDACTED] });
   132→
   133→      // Invalidate derived generations for the source
   134→      if (data.based_on) {
   135→        queryClient.invalidateQueries({
   136→          [REDACTED](data.based_on),
   137→        });
   138→      }
   139→    },
   140→
   141→    onError: (error) => {
   142→      normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'usePromoteVariantToGeneration', toastTitle: 'Failed to create new image from variant' });
   143→    },
   144→  });
   145→};
   146→
   147→// NOTE: Default export removed - use named export { usePromoteVariantToGeneration } instead
   148→
```

## Summary: VariantSelector Integration in Video Lightbox

Based on my thorough analysis of the codebase, here's the exact flow of VariantSelector integration:

---

### 1. **VideoLightbox → Render Model → VariantSelector Path**

**File:** `src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/VideoLightbox.tsx`

The VideoLightbox builds a render model that creates two possible panel contents:
- If in **video edit mode**: `VideoEditPanel`
- Otherwise: `InfoPanel` (line 74-92 in render model)

```
VideoLightbox
  → useVideoLightboxRenderModel()
    → buildVideoLightboxControlsPanel()
      → returns InfoPanel (when not in edit mode)
```

---

### 2. **How Variant Data Flows to InfoPanel**

**File:** `src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/hooks/useVideoLightboxRenderModel.tsx` (lines 74-92)

The render model passes `sharedState` which contains all variant data:

```typescript
<InfoPanel
  // ...
  taskDetailsData={editModel.adjustedTaskDetailsData}
  // Variant data comes via context, not props:
  // useVideoLightboxSharedState builds sharedState.variants
/>
```

The **key insight**: Variant data is NOT passed as props. Instead, it's provided via **LightboxStateContext** that wraps the entire lightbox.

---

### 3. **InfoPanel VariantSelector Rendering**

**File:** `src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/components/InfoPanel.tsx` (lines 237-265)

The InfoPanel gets variant state from context:

```typescript
const {
  variants,
  activeVariant,
  primaryVariant,
  handleVariantSelect: onVariantSelect,
  handleMakePrimary: onMakePrimary,
  isLoadingVariants,
  variantsSectionRef,
  pendingTaskCount,
  unviewedVariantCount,
  onMarkAllViewed,
  handlePromoteToGeneration: onPromoteToGeneration,
  isPromoting,
  handleDeleteVariant: onDeleteVariant,
  onLoadVariantSettings,
  onLoadVariantImages,
  currentSegmentImages,
} = useLightboxVariantsSafe();
```

Then renders the VariantSelector in the scrollable content area:

```typescript
<div ref={variantsSectionRef} className={cn("border-t border-border", variantPadding)}>
  <VariantSelector
    variants={variants}
    activeVariantId={activeVariant?.id || null}
    onVariantSelect={onVariantSelect}
    onMakePrimary={onMakePrimary}
    isLoading={isLoadingVariants}
    onPromoteToGeneration={onPromoteToGeneration}
    isPromoting={isPromoting}
    onDeleteVariant={onDeleteVariant}
    onLoadVariantSettings={onLoadVariantSettings}
    onLoadVariantImages={onLoadVariantImages}
    currentSegmentImages={currentSegmentImages}
    readOnly={readOnly}
  />
</div>
```

**Also in EditPanelLayout:** `src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/components/EditPanelLayout.tsx` (lines 217-237) uses the same pattern.

---

### 4. **useVariantSelection vs useVariants**

**useVariants** (`src/shared/hooks/useVariants.ts`):
- Raw hook that fetches variants from DB
- Manages `activeVariantId` state
- Provides `setActiveVariantId()`, `setPrimaryVariant()`, `deleteVariant()`
- Does NOT mark as viewed

**useVariantSelection** (`src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/hooks/useVariantSelection.ts`):
- **Wrapper** around `useVariants`
- Adds **mark-as-viewed behavior** on variant selection
- Tracks which variants have been marked to avoid duplicates
- Handles initial variant setup from props
- Returns wrapped `setActiveVariantId()` that fires `markViewed()` before calling raw setter

```typescript
const setActiveVariantId = useCallback((variantId: string) => {
  // Mark variant as viewed when selected (fire-and-forget)
  if (variantId) {
    const generationId = getGenerationId(media);
    markViewed({ variantId, generationId: generationId ?? undefined });
  }
  rawSetActiveVariantId(variantId);
}, [rawSetActiveVariantId, markViewed, media]);
```

---

### 5. **usePromoteVariantToGeneration Hook**

**File:** `src/shared/hooks/usePromoteVariantToGeneration.ts`

This is a **React Query mutation** that:
1. Fetches the variant from `generation_variants` table
2. Determines if it's video or image based on file extension
3. Creates a **new generation** record with:
   - Same location/thumbnail as variant
   - `based_on` = source generation ID (lineage tracking)
   - Cleaned params (strips `generation_id`, `source_task_id`)
   - Sets `source: 'variant_promotion'`, `source_variant_id`, `source_generation_id`
4. DB trigger auto-creates the primary variant for the new generation
5. Invalidates React Query caches for generations and derived generations

Used by VariantSelector when user clicks "Make new image" button.

---

### 6. **EditVideoPage State Management** (lines 1-150)

**File:** `src/tools/edit-video/pages/EditVideoPage.tsx`

Key state setup:

```typescript
// Persisted media selection hook

} = useEditToolMediaPersistence({

const [lightboxVariantId, setLightboxVariantId] = useState<string | null>(null);

// Transform variant to GenerationRow (converts variant data to format lightbox expects)
const transformVariantToGeneration = useCallback(
  (media: GeneratedImageWithMetadata): GenerationRow =>
    variantToGenerationRow(media, 'video', selectedProjectId || ''),

  12,
  true,
  {
    variantsOnly: true,
    toolType: TOOL_TYPE,
    mediaType: 'video',
    parentsOnly: true,
  }
);
```

### 7. **Key Integration Points for InlineEditVideoView**

To wire VariantSelector into InlineEditVideoView:

**Current setup** (`InlineEditVideoView.tsx` lines 41-48):
```typescript
interface InlineEditVideoViewProps {
  media: GenerationRow;              // The video to edit
  onClose: () => void;
  onVideoSaved?: (newVideoUrl: string) => Promise<void>;
  onNavigateToGeneration?: (generationId: string) => Promise<void>;
  initialSegments?: PortionSelection[];
  onSegmentsChange?: (segments: PortionSelection[]) => void;
}
```

**To add VariantSelector, you would need to:**

1. **Get variant data**: Call `useVariants()` with `media.id` (generation ID)
2. **Wrap selection with mark-as-viewed**: Use `useVariantSelection()` wrapper
3. **Provide variant handlers**: Wire up:
   - `onVariantSelect` → switches active variant
   - `onMakePrimary` → uses `useVariants().setPrimaryVariant()`
   - `onPromoteToGeneration` → uses `usePromoteVariantToGeneration()`
   - `onLoadVariantSettings` → loads variant params into form
   - `onLoadVariantImages` → (segment-specific, probably skip for now)
   - `onDeleteVariant` → uses `useVariants().deleteVariant()`

4. **Render VariantSelector** as a section in the panel (like InfoPanel does)

---

### 8. **LightboxStateContext Integration Pattern**

The MediaLightbox and VideoLightbox use a **single context** to avoid prop drilling:

```typescript
<LightboxStateProvider value={renderModel.lightboxStateValue}>
  <VideoEditProvider value={editModel.videoEditValue}>
    <LightboxShell>
      <LightboxLayout />
    </LightboxShell>
  </VideoEditProvider>
</LightboxStateProvider>
```

The `lightboxStateValue` includes:
- `core`: onClose, readOnly, projectId
- `media`: video/image data
- **`variants`**: all variant handlers and state
- `navigation`: prev/next handlers

Components deep in the tree call `useLightboxVariantsSafe()` to access without prop drilling.

---

### **Summary Table: Integration Points**

| Component | Purpose | Key Props/Hooks |
|-----------|---------|-----------------|
| **VideoLightbox** | Entry point | Builds render model, wraps with context |
| **useVideoLightboxRenderModel** | Decides panel content | Returns InfoPanel or VideoEditPanel |
| **InfoPanel** | Info view | Renders TaskDetails + VariantSelector |
| **EditPanelLayout** | Edit view | Renders forms + VariantSelector |
| **VariantSelector** | UI component | Grid of variant thumbnails + actions |
| **useVariants** | Data fetch & mutation | Fetches variants, setPrimary, deleteVariant |
| **useVariantSelection** | Wrapper with side-effect | Adds mark-as-viewed on select |
| **usePromoteVariantToGeneration** | Variant promotion | Creates new generation from variant |
| **LightboxStateContext** | Avoids prop drilling | Provides all variant state/handlers |

---

This design ensures:
✓ No prop drilling through deep component trees
✓ Variant selection automatically marks as viewed
✓ Consistent UI between InfoPanel and EditPanelLayout
✓ Easy reuse in other tools (like InlineEditVideoView)
